Motivation: 现有稀疏因果注意力通常基于序列局部性假设（相邻token更易访问），但作者发现固定块因果注意力中，即使相邻token也可能在每一层的注意力图中被断开连接，形成边界伪影。这种局部性与可达性的不匹配尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 通过结构依赖集形式化边界伪影，证明在固定块因果掩码下目标表示只能依赖其块前缀内的token；2) 推导相位条件覆盖函数，揭示可达性同时依赖于源-目标距离和目标在块内的偏移量；3) 提出边界桥接注意力，在不增加额外参数的情况下在块边界附近添加辅助因果边。

Result: 在1024 token可控实验中，修复效果集中在覆盖对齐的诊断指标上；在Qwen2.5-7B（8K token）固定检查点探针实验中，验证了相同的覆盖不可比性模式。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一套基于理论指导的块稀疏因果注意力局部性-可达性不匹配诊断框架，包含相位条件覆盖分析和最小化构造性修复方法。

Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

</details>


### [115] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiRe-OPD提出了一种在轨迹级别和token级别联合调整监督信号的在线策略蒸馏方法，先过滤低质量轨迹，再对保留轨迹内的token进行软重加权，实现更细粒度的优化，在多种设置下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏方法正从全轨迹KL监督转向选择性训练范式，但大多仅在单一粒度（轨迹或token）上进行选择，缺乏联合优化。为更细粒度地控制蒸馏过程，作者提出同时从轨迹和token两个层面调整监督信号，以提升蒸馏效果。

Method: FiRe-OPD采用两阶段策略：首先过滤低质量的rollout轨迹；然后在保留的轨迹内对每个token进行软重加权，强调信息量大的token，并通过软权重机制替代硬token选择，减少信息损失并增强优化稳定性。

Result: 在强到弱、单教师和多教师设置下均验证了有效性，在AIME 2024上比强到弱基线提升6.25，在多教师Miner任务上提升18.81。

Conclusion: FiRe-OPD通过轨迹过滤与token软重加权的联合优化，实现了更细粒度的在线策略蒸馏，显著优于现有的token级OPD方法。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization. We validate the effectiveness of FiRe-OPD across strong-to-weak, single-teacher, and multi-teacher settings, and demonstrate its superiority over recent token-level OPD methods ( (e.g., +6.25 on AIME 2024 in strong-to-weak, +18.81 on Miner in multi-teacher). Our code is available at https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD.

</details>


### [116] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ψ-Bench基准，评估LLM在真实交互场景中通过对话说服用户的主动个性化能力。测试10个前沿模型发现，即使最先进的模型在说服力上仍有显著提升空间，而提供用户画像信息可带来平均18.24%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化语言代理主要定位为被动响应用户偏好，缺乏主动与用户交互、提供建议或指导的能力，因此需要系统评估LLM在真实交互中的主动个性化能力。

Method: 设计Ψ-Bench基准，包含三个涉及说服的真实世界交互场景；通过对话历史中的显式用户画像赋予模拟客户端个性特征；评估10个前沿LLM的表现。

Result: 大多数模型能生成连贯合理的论点，但即使最先进模型在说服力上仍有较大提升空间；提供用户画像信息平均带来18.24%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 个性化敏感的说服能力是评估和开发更主动的个性化LLM代理的一个具有挑战性且实用的方向。

Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

</details>


### [117] [Binary Road Surface Classification Using Machine Learning on Production Vehicle Signals During Cruising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02762)
*Vishal Hariharan,Salar Basiri,Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用机器学习方法（特征工程和端到端方法），通过车辆动力学信号（轮速、扭矩、加速度、转向角、横摆角速度）的滑动窗口批处理，在巡航工况下对道路表面状态（干燥/湿滑 vs 冰雪）进行二分类，解决了传统基于动力学递归估计器在低滑移率场景下失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于滑移率-摩擦系数斜率的动力学递推估计方法在车辆巡航（低滑移）时失效，因为现有传感器无法精确测量或估计微小滑移，而微小滑移对于区分不同路面至关重要。因此需要利用机器学习挖掘巡航状态下车辆信号与路面条件之间的关联。

Method: 采用两种数据驱动框架：（1）基于特征的框架，提取车辆动力学信号统计特征；（2）端到端数据驱动框架。使用滑动窗口方法批处理短时缓冲窗口内的轮速、轮扭矩、纵向加速度、转向角和横摆角速度信号，输入机器学习模块进行路面状态二分类（抓地类：干燥/潮湿 vs 滑移类：冰雪）。

Result: 在公共道路数据的验证中，数据驱动方法能够在巡航工况下正确识别路面表面状况，展示了数据驱动的摩擦相关状态估计器在车辆轮胎动力学领域的应用前景。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的路面摩擦状态估计方法能够克服传统方法在巡航工况下的局限性，为车辆预警和干预控制系统提供实时路面滑移程度或峰值抓地力估计的关键输入。

Abstract: Knowledge of real-time road slipperiness, or even better, a refined estimate of peak grip potential, is a critical input for vehicle warning and intervention control systems. Typically, friction is estimated through dynamics-based recursive estimators by calculating the slip slope; however, its efficacy is heavily constrained by the vehicle dynamic scenario. When the vehicle is cruising and there is little to no slip, these methods become ineffective due to the inability of present-day production-grade sensors, such as wheel speed sensors, and methods to either measure or accurately estimate micro slip, which is crucial for distinguishing different surfaces. To address this challenge, the correlation between vehicle signals and road surface condition during cruising needs to be uncovered using machine learning. In this paper, a feature-based framework and an end-to-end data-driven framework are used to correlate the statistics of vehicle dynamics behavior with the condition of the road surface and perform binary classification into grip, dry or damp, and slip, snow or ice, conditions. A sliding-window approach is adopted to batch a short buffered window of wheel speeds, wheel torques, longitudinal acceleration, steering angle, and yaw rate, which are fed into a machine learning module for predicting the road state. Validation results on public-road data show scenarios where the data-driven method identifies the road surface correctly even during cruising, showing promise for accurate data-driven friction-related state estimators in the field of tire and vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Representational Capacity: Geometric Limits on Feature Representation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02765)
*Alexander Guha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性表示和叠加假设的理论框架，通过嵌入矩阵的余弦相似度分布估计模型对正交性的容忍偏差ε，并修正Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理得到新的表示容量公式（取决于k/d而非k），将预测误差降低两个数量级。研究发现模型可分为高ε（缺乏近正交结构）和低ε（保持近正交结构）两类，且大模型倾向于更紧的正交约束而非最大化容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型维度d_model是Transformer语言模型的基本超参数，但其对特征表示几何边界的设定作用尚未充分探索。本文旨在从线性表示和叠加假设出发，建立一个估计潜在空间中可支持近正交方向数量的理论框架。

Method: 1) 将嵌入矩阵作为近正交约束的可测量代理，通过成对余弦相似度分布中语义关系与偶然相似性的边界估计偏差ε；2) 在数十个开源模型上应用该指标，识别高ε和低ε两类模型；3) 修正Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理，推导出新的容量公式：近正交方向数量取决于向量数与维数之比(k/d)，而非原始计数k。

Result: 1) 发现了模型的两类划分：高ε模型嵌入缺乏近正交结构，低ε模型保持该结构；2) 标准JL引理严重低估了训练表示的填充效率，修正后的公式（基于k/d）将预测误差降低两个数量级且无需额外参数；3) 表示容量对ε呈指数敏感，大模型偏好更紧的正交约束而非最大化原始容量。

Conclusion: 定义了表示容量作为潜在空间中特征和嵌入可区分方向数量的上界。大模型倾向于更紧的正交约束这一模式可能与稳定性-容量权衡、可用概念上限或模型规模的混淆因素有关，留待未来工作探索。

Abstract: Model dimension ($d_{model}$) is a fundamental hyperparameter in transformer language models, yet its role in setting the geometric limits of feature representation remains under-explored. Grounded in the Linear Representation and Superposition Hypotheses - which propose that models encode features as near-orthogonal directions in latent space - we develop a framework for estimating how many such directions a model can support. We first establish the embedding matrix as a measurable proxy for near-orthogonality constraints across the latent space: the boundary between meaningful token relationships and incidental similarity in the pairwise cosine similarity distribution gives a concrete estimate of the model's accepted deviation $\varepsilon$ from perfect orthogonality. Applying this metric across dozens of open-source models reveals two classes: models with high $\varepsilon$ whose embeddings lack near-orthogonal structure, and models with low $\varepsilon$ that maintain it. We then show that the standard Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma greatly underestimates the packing efficiency of trained representations, and derive an adjusted capacity formula in which the number of near-orthogonal directions depends on the ratio of vectors to dimensions ($k/d$) rather than the raw count - a single modification that cuts prediction error by two orders of magnitude with no extra parameters. Combining these results, we define representational capacity as an upper bound on the number of distinguishable directions available for features and embeddings in a model's latent space. Capacity is exponentially sensitive to $\varepsilon$, and larger models favor tighter orthogonality constraints over maximizing raw capacity - a pattern compatible with several explanations (a stability-capacity trade-off, a ceiling on usable concepts, or confounds with model scale) that we leave to future work.

</details>


### [119] [QUIVER: Quantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations in Large ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02785)
*Aritra Bal,Michael Binder,Markus Klute,Benedikt Maier,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUIVER提出了一种利用量子Fisher信息矩阵作为新型多模态特征增强方法，从变分量子电路中提取几何驱动的互补特征，在QM9和JetClass基准上提升了标准模型性能，且无需容错量子硬件即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型机器学习模型能从多模态输入中获益，但经典特征增强难以捕捉量子态流形上的高阶统计结构。现有方法缺少一种真正互补而非冗余的模态，尤其是基于量子信息论几何视角的特征。

Method: 使用变分量子电路（VQC）训练执行目标任务，从量子Fisher信息矩阵中提取几何驱动、基无关的量子Fisher视图，将其作为辅助模态融合到经典模型架构中，通过针对性修改基础架构实现信息注入。

Result: 在QM9分子性质预测和JetClass喷注味标记两个跨领域基准数据集上，QUIVER均提升了标准性能指标，证明了量子几何特征在实际机器学习任务中的可测量价值。

Conclusion: 量子Fisher视图是一种与经典特征真正互补的模态，能从模拟变分电路中提取有价值的信息，在容错量子硬件成熟之前即可为经典机器学习任务带来提升，且方法具有领域无关性。

Abstract: Large machine learning models benefit substantially from multimodal inputs that provide a complementary view of the same example. We introduce QUIVER (QUantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations, a paradigm that enriches classical data-driven features with a quantum Fisher view: a geometrically motivated, basis-independent summary of higher-order correlations captured by a variational quantum circuit (VQC) trained to perform the same task. Unlike classical feature augmentation, the quantum Fisher information matrix encodes the intrinsic geometry of the learned quantum state manifold. While this feature map, motivated by quantum information theory, is ordinarily non-trivial to model classically, it can surface statistical structure that additional classical data or model capacity finds difficult to learn. This makes the quantum Fisher view a genuinely complementary modality rather than a redundant one. We demonstrate that QUIVER improves standard performance metrics on two benchmark datasets from very different fields: QM9 for predicting molecule properties, and JetClass for predicting jet flavor at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). The core contribution, however, is domain-agnostic: the quantum Fisher view can be fused into a broad class of model architectures via targeted modifications to the base architecture, to incorporate information about the quantum geometry of the problem. These results demonstrate that quantum-geometric features, extracted from simulated variational circuits, can deliver measurable value for standard machine learning tasks, well before the advent of fault-tolerant quantum hardware.

</details>


### [120] [Qift: Shift-Friendly No-Zero W2 Post-Training Quantization for Rotated W2A4/KV4 LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02823)
*Chi-Wei Huang,Chia-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Qift，一种面向Hadamard旋转后W2A4/KV4量化的固定无零权重层级集{+/-0.5, +/-1.5}（等价于{+/-1, +/-3}），基于预训练权重的近似零中心高斯化分布特性，替代标准{ -2,-1,0,+1}层级集，无需训练、无需码本、无需分组网格，在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上显著提升W2A4困惑度、下游准确率和GPTQ残差行为，缩小了与W3A4的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准W2量化层级集{-2,-1,0,+1}在激进的W2A4/KV4设置下常发生坍塌，表明W2A4失效不仅是位宽问题也是重建层级问题。本文研究Hadamard旋转量化流水线中两比特权重的标量层级几何，并基于预训练权重已接近零中心、Hadamard旋转主要高斯化其标准化形状的观察，提出更优的固定层级设计。

Method: 通过分析LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B共224个线性模块，发现预训练权重近似零中心，Hadamard旋转使超值峰度和Q-Q误差下降数量级。基于此零中心高斯化源模型，提出Qift固定无零层级集{+/-0.5, +/-1.5}（缩放到{+/-1, +/-3}），以及2的幂次变体{+/-1, +/-4}。该方法无训练、无学习码本、无分组网格、无零点，保留标准每通道缩放。通过尺度不变比率分析确定有效内外质心比范围0.25~0.33，解释现有方法的优劣。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，无零层级集在纯W2A4困惑度、L层混合W2/W4困惑度、下游准确率和GPTQ残差行为上一致优于标准W2层级集。在L=16混合精度下，显著缩小与W3A4的差距，同时保持一半Transformer层为两比特精度。

Conclusion: Qift提供了一种简单、源感知、易于部署的替代方案，避免了更复杂的学习式W2码本，证明了W2A4量化的改进不仅依赖于位宽，更依赖于合理的重建层级几何设计。

Abstract: Two-bit weight quantization is attractive for memory-efficient LLM inference, but the standard W2 level set {-2,-1,0,+1} often collapses under aggressive W2A4/KV4 settings. We study the scalar level-set geometry of two-bit weights in a Hadamard-rotated quantization pipeline. Conventional asymmetric W2 substantially improves over the standard level set, indicating that W2A4 failure is not only a bit-width problem but also a reconstruction-level problem. Across all 224 linear modules in each of LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, pretrained weights are already nearly zero-centered, while Hadamard rotation primarily Gaussianizes their standardized shape: excess kurtosis and Q-Q error drop by orders of magnitude. Based on this approximate zero-centered Gaussian-like source model, we propose Qift, a fixed no-zero W2 level set for rotated W2A4/KV4 inference. The main level set is {+/-0.5, +/-1.5}, equivalently {+/-1, +/-3} under a half-scale reparameterization; a power-of-two variant uses {+/-1, +/-4} for sign-and-shift decoded weight application. Qift redesigns the fixed two-bit code-to-level mapping and is training-free, learned-codebook-free, group-grid-free, and zero-point-free, retaining the standard per-channel scale. A scale-invariant ratio analysis identifies an effective inner/outer centroid ratio range of 0.25 to 0.33, explaining why mirror no-zero (MNZ), Lloyd, NF2, and PoT-MNZ perform well while {+/-1, +/-2} does not. On both models, the no-zero level sets consistently improve pure W2A4 perplexity, L-layer mixed W2/W4 perplexity, downstream accuracy, and GPTQ residual behavior over the standard W2 level set. At L=16 mixed precision, they substantially narrow the gap to W3A4 while keeping half of the transformer layers at two-bit precision, giving a simple, source-aware, and deployment-friendly alternative to more complex learned W2 codebooks.

</details>


### [121] [Mitigating Spurious Correlations with Memorization-Guided Dataset De-Biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02830)
*Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段样本评分函数，通过解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态来分别评估其难度，并基于此设计算法筛选信息样本以缓解虚假相关性。仅用10%训练数据训练的ERM模型即可超越最先进的去偏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中常存在与目标标签无因果关系的虚假相关性，导致模型依赖这些伪模式而误分类少数样本。现有子集选择方法依赖的样本评分函数受虚假特征影响，无法准确捕捉核心因果特征的重要性。

Method: 提出两阶段样本评分函数，首先解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态，然后分别评估两类特征上的样本难度。基于该度量设计算法，优先筛选具有信息性的样本（包括有/无虚假相关性的样本）。

Result: 在仅使用10%原始训练数据的情况下，标准ERM模型在所选子集上训练即可超越最先进的去偏方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦特征学习动态并分别评估样本难度，可以有效缓解虚假相关性，且无需组标签即可实现高效的数据选择与模型去偏。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often contain spurious correlations that are not causally related to the target label. When such correlations dominate the majority of training samples, models tend to rely on them, leading to misclassification of minority samples that do not exhibit the same spurious patterns. While a potential approach is to select subsets of data to better represent the minority samples, this may require access to group labels, which are typically unknown. Furthermore, as we demonstrate, widely used sample scoring functions in the invariant subset or coreset selection literature largely depend on spurious features and therefore fail to accurately capture the importance or difficulty of core, causally relevant features. Accordingly, we propose to mitigate spurious correlations by developing a two-stage sample scoring function that disentangles the learning dynamics of core and spurious features and evaluates their difficulty separately. Based on our proposed metric, we introduce a new algorithm to find and prioritize informative samples both with and without spurious correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a standard ERM model trained on our selected samples achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art debiasing techniques, while requiring as little as 10\% of the original training data.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Coherent Representations: A Topological Approach to Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02841)
*Sigurd Gaukstad,Melvin Vaupel,Valdemar Kargård Olsen,Erik Hermansen,Benjamin Dunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"一致性"(coherence)这一几何性质，用于约束神经网络特征使其具有可解释性。一致性要求特征在样本空间中形成连续的几何簇，而非稀疏但散乱的激活。作者证明一致性保证样本空间与特征空间具有兼容的拓扑结构，并基于Fréchet方差设计了可微的目标函数Coh。实验在自编码器和BERT词嵌入中验证了该方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中学到的表示中，单个神经元往往缺乏可解释性，可能对分散的、不相关的输入产生响应。受大脑中网格细胞和头朝向细胞等神经元的启发——这些神经元对状态空间的连续区域做出响应——作者希望将这种几何约束引入人工神经网络，使学习到的特征具有内在的可解释性。

Method: 定义"一致性"：非负矩阵中每行（样本）关注几何上聚类的列（特征），反之亦然，且每个样本由某个特征描述，每个特征被某些样本需要。证明一致性保证了样本和特征的Vietoris-Rips过滤之间存在有界交错，从而共享兼容的拓扑结构。基于Fréchet方差设计可微目标函数Coh来训练中强制执行一致性。

Result: 在合成数据集和旋转MNIST数据集上的自编码器实验，以及在语言数据的BERT词嵌入实验中验证了Coh的有效性。

Conclusion: 与稀疏性（约束特征激活多少个样本）不同，一致性约束特征激活哪些样本，要求几何连通性而非仅稀有性，从而产生不仅可解释的特征，而且可解释的特征空间。

Abstract: Deep neural networks learn representations where individual features often lack interpretable meaning; a single neuron may activate for scattered, unrelated inputs. We introduce coherence, a geometric property inspired by neural coding in the brain, where neurons like grid cells and head direction cells respond to contiguous regions of state space. A non-negative matrix is coherent if each row (sample) attends to geometrically clustered columns (features) and vice versa, and in addition every sample is well described by some feature and every feature is needed by some sample. We prove that coherent matrices induce a bounded interleaving between the Vietoris-Rips filtrations of samples and features, guaranteeing that both spaces share compatible topological structure. This geometric constraint facilitates interpretability. For example, if data lies on a circle, coherent features must tile that circle into contiguous arcs. We introduce Coh, a differentiable objective function based on Fréchet variance that enforces coherence during training. Unlike sparsity, which bounds how many samples a feature activates on, coherence bounds which samples, requiring geometric connectivity rather than only rarity. This yields not just interpretable features but an interpretable feature space. We validate Coh in an auto-encoder using synthetic and rotated MNIST datasets and in a token embedding of BERT using language data.

</details>


### [123] [Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow for Lightweight Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02842)
*Yixian Shen,Zhiheng Yang,Qi Bi,Changshuo Wang,Shuai Wang,Jia-Hong Huang,George Floros,Prayag Tiwari,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecFlow提出了一种基于频谱渐进式思维流的多模态空间推理框架，通过将中间视觉表示压缩到固定大小的离散余弦空间中，并利用无分类器引导实现文本与视觉状态的协同演进，在保持推理性能的同时将计算和KV缓存成本降低最多2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多模态空间推理中依赖长链的中间文本和视觉思维，导致视觉token累积和密集跨模态注意力带来巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 将中间视觉思维表示为固定大小的离散余弦空间（DCT），利用能量压缩保留全局布局和关系结构，仅在需要高空间精度时引入高频细节；采用无分类器引导实现自回归文本思维驱动基于流的视觉工作空间更新，无需扩展上下文。

Result: 在保持竞争性或更优推理性能的同时，计算和KV缓存成本降低最多2.1倍。

Conclusion: SpecFlow通过固定大小的视觉工作空间和流式更新机制，实现了与推理深度无关的稳定延迟和内存占用，是一种高效的多模态空间推理框架。

Abstract: Multimodal spatial reasoning often relies on long chains of intermediate textual and visual thoughts, where accumulating visual tokens and dense cross-modal attention incur substantial computation and memory overhead. To address this challenge, we propose Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow (SpecFlow), a novel lightweight multimodal spatial reasoning framework that represents intermediate visual thoughts in a fixed-size discrete cosine space. By exploiting strong energy compaction, SpecFlow preserves global layout and relational structure while introducing high-frequency details only when increased spatial precision is required. To align visual state evolution with linguistic intent, classifier-free guidance enables autoregressive textual thoughts to steer flow-based updates of the visual workspace/state without expanding the context. As a result, SpecFlow maintains a bounded visual workspace whose updates depend only on the current visual state and accumulated textual trace, enabling long-horizon inference with stable latency and memory usage independent of reasoning depth. Empirical results show that SpecFlow achieves competitive or superior reasoning performance while reducing computation and KV cache costs by up to 2.1 times.

</details>


### [124] [A Systematic Evaluation of Current Architectures in Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02849)
*Vinicius Bortolini,Gilson Adamczuk Oliveira,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Matheus Henrique Dal Molin Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了混合方法在风电区间预测中的应用，重点探讨了深度学习、模态分解与统计方法的结合。研究发现，将混合模型与VMD、EEMD等分解技术集成可提高预测精度与可靠性；区间构建多采用双模型策略分别预测上下界；评估指标需平衡覆盖率和区间宽度。同时指出了标准化评估缺失、计算复杂度高等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电固有的不确定性对电力系统集成构成挑战，区间风速预测能够量化这种不确定性，但目前关于混合方法在风电区间预测中的系统性综述尚不充分，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，运用Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA)主题建模指导论文筛选，识别研究模式与趋势；重点分析混合模型（深度学习、模态分解、统计方法）在区间预测中的组合策略。

Result: (1) 集成VMD、EEMD等分解技术能缩小预测区间且不牺牲覆盖率；(2) 多数研究采用双模型策略独立预测上下界；(3) 常用输入分解技术为EMD、EEMD或VMD，模型多为LSTM或ELM；(4) 区间质量评估需平衡覆盖率和区间宽度。

Conclusion: 区间风电预测对风电运行具有重要价值，但当前存在标准化评估指标缺失、计算复杂度高、实际验证不足等挑战，未来需提升模型鲁棒性和决策支持能力。

Abstract: Interval wind speed forecasting is essential for the efficient integration of wind energy into power systems, as it accounts for the inherent uncertainty of wind resources. This study presents a systematic literature review focused on hybrid approaches to interval forecasting of wind generation, exploring the combination of deep learning, modal decomposition, and statistical methods. To guide the paper selection, Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) was applied for topic modeling, enabling the identification of patterns and research trends. The findings emphasize that integrating hybrid models with decomposition techniques-such as Variational Mode Decomposition (VMD) and Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD)-enhances forecast accuracy and reliability by narrowing prediction intervals without compromising coverage. Regarding interval construction, most studies adopt a dual-model strategy, independently forecasting the lower and upper bounds. Input data are commonly decomposed using techniques like EMD, EEMD, or VMD, which extract frequency-based components. These components serve as inputs to models such as LSTM or ELM, trained separately for each bound. This approach allows for targeted modeling of uncertainty, improving flexibility and precision, Interval quality is typically evaluated through metrics that balance coverage and interval width. The review also highlights challenges, including the lack of standardized evaluation metrics, computational complexity, and limited real-world validation. Overall, the study reinforces the value of interval forecasting for wind energy operations and offers insights for advancing model robustness and decision-making.

</details>


### [125] [RESCAST-100K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Cross-Domain Residential Load and Indoor Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02852)
*Jainam Dhruva,Yousaf Raza,A. B. Siddique,Simone Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RESCAST-100K是一个大规模住宅预测基准，包含约10万个EnergyPlus模拟的美国住宅数据，支持跨领域迁移学习、域适应和零样本泛化的系统评估，覆盖总负荷、HVAC负荷和室内温度三个耦合目标，并整合了五个真实数据集用于sim-to-real评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住宅数据集覆盖范围窄，缺乏支持结构化跨域评估的基准，限制了域适应和迁移学习在住宅预测中的进展。

Method: 构建RESCAST-100K基准：基于ResStock的EnergyPlus模拟生成约10万美国住宅的15分钟时间序列（总负荷、HVAC负荷、室内温度），提供可配置的领域实例化接口（地理、气候区、墙体构造、供暖设备），整合五个真实数据集，并基准测试循环、注意力、MLP-mixer等架构的零样本性能。

Result: 跨注意力模型和MLP-mixer模型在域偏移、缺失输入条件下持续优于循环网络和经典transformer基线。

Conclusion: RESCAST-100K旨在推动机器学习和建筑分析社区在家庭、社区和电网尺度上的跨域住宅预测研究。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of residential energy load and indoor temperature is essential for home energy management systems, grid-level demand response, and community energy efficiency efforts. Domain adaptation and transfer learning have shown promise for improving forecasting accuracy under data heterogeneity and scarcity commonly seen in residential settings. However, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive residential datasets: existing benchmarks are narrow in target coverage and rarely support structured cross-domain evaluation. We introduce RESCAST-100K, a large-scale residential forecasting benchmark for studying cross-domain generalization. It provides a configuration-driven interface that instantiates source and target domains along interpretable axes, including geography, climate zone, wall construction, and heating equipment, enabling systematic evaluation of transfer learning, domain adaptation, and zero-shot generalization under controlled domain shifts. The benchmark covers approximately 100,000 EnergyPlus-simulated U.S. homes derived from ResStock, with 15-minute time series for three coupled targets per home: total load, HVAC load, and indoor temperature. These are paired with weather channels, HVAC setpoints, and over 40 static building covariates. RESCAST-100K also integrates five real-world residential datasets under a unified schema, supporting sim-to-real evaluation on the same tasks. We benchmark recurrent, attention-based, and MLP-mixer architectures for zero-shot performance across domains, missing-input conditions, and forecasting tasks. Cross-attention and MLP-mixer models consistently outperform recurrent and classical transformer baselines under domain shift. RESCAST-100K is intended to aid the machine learning and building analytics communities advance cross-domain residential forecasting at home, community, and grid scale.

</details>


### [126] [GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Yifan Yang,Ruijie Zhang,Xinling Yu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRZO优化器，通过组相对归一化聚合每个样本的损失，在不增加前向成本的情况下将有效梯度方向数从1提升到批次大小，显著降低方差并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化是微调大语言模型的内存高效替代方案，但由于梯度估计的高方差，其部署受到限制。

Method: GRZO对每个小批量样本生成一个伪独立扰动，通过组相对归一化聚合每个样本的损失，从而将有效梯度方向计数从1提升到批次大小，且不增加额外前向成本。

Result: 在RoBERTa-large、Llama3-8B和OPT-13B上，GRZO在Llama3-8B上相比MeZO平均准确率提升+3.0，峰值GPU内存降低23%；作为MeZO核心的替代品，它使稀疏、低秩和量化ZO变体平均提升+6.0。

Conclusion: GRZO在方向无偏且方差随批次大小成比例缩小，具有比MeZO更紧的非凸收敛界。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization is a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models, but its deployment is limited by the high variance of gradient estimation. We propose GRZO, a Group-Relative Zeroth-Order optimizer that draws one pseudo-independent perturbation per mini-batch example and aggregates the per-example losses through group-relative normalization, raising the effective gradient-direction count from one to the batch size at no additional forward cost while preserving inference-level memory. We prove that GRZO is directionally unbiased with variance shrinking proportionally to the batch size, yielding a tighter nonconvex convergence bound than MeZO. Across RoBERTa-large, Llama3-8B, and OPT-13B over multiple tasks, GRZO improves average accuracy on Llama3-8B by $+3.0$ over MeZO at $23\%$ lower peak GPU memory; as a drop-in replacement for the MeZO core, it lifts sparse, low-rank, and quantized ZO variants by $+6.0$ on average.

</details>


### [127] [Forgetting is Not Erasure: Recovering Latent Knowledge via Transport Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02860)
*Archie Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 灾难性遗忘并非完全由特征丢失导致，很大一部分可归因于内部计算阶段的接口漂移。通过缝合评估协议和紧凑的传输键（transport key），在顺序训练后能恢复大部分早期任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战灾难性遗忘完全由表征丢失导致的强观点，探究遗忘是否部分源于网络内部阶段间的接口漂移而非特征永久性擦除。

Method: 提出缝合评估协议：将更新后网络的早期计算与其前身的晚期计算组合，通过紧凑的、任务特定的传输键（从少量配对锚点激活估计的接口对齐算子）进行中介，在分割CIFAR-100和ResNet/ViT架构上验证。

Result: 在分割CIFAR-100上，传输键在顺序训练任务B后恢复了大部分任务A的性能；在紧凑视觉Transformer上也观察到类似恢复模式。

Conclusion: 持续学习可能需要更好的机制来索引和重新访问潜在计算，而不仅仅是防止权重变化的方法。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often framed as a representational problem: after sequential training, a model appears to lose the features that supported performance on earlier tasks. We challenge the stronger form of this view. Across controlled continual-learning settings, we find that a significant portion of apparent forgetting can be attributed to interface drift between internal stages rather than permanent erasure of task-relevant computation. We study this phenomenon through a stitched evaluation protocol that combines early computation from a post-update network with late computation from its predecessor, optionally mediated by a compact, task-specific transport key. We describe transport keys at a systems level as compact interface-alignment operators estimated from a small set of paired anchor activations and evaluated through model stitching. On split CIFAR-100 with a ResNet-style network, transport keys recover most of the original Task A performance after sequential training on Task B. On a compact vision transformer, we observe a similar recovery pattern. These results suggest that continual learning may require better mechanisms for indexing and re-accessing latent computations, not only methods that prevent weight change.

</details>


### [128] [RRISE: Robust Radius Inference via a Surrogate Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02876)
*Jong-Ik Park,Shreyas Chaudhari,Carlee Joe-Wong,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRISE通过训练一个学习代理（surrogate）将随机平滑的认证过程压缩为单次前向传播，利用共形校准提供可部署验证的保守认证半径，在几乎不损失认证精度的前提下，将每次查询所需的蒙特卡洛采样从10^4次替代为单次代理前向传播，并在CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上相较先前离线代理方法提升1.23-1.91倍认证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑虽然能提供架构无关的ℓ2鲁棒性认证，但其依赖逐输入的蒙特卡洛采样，计算成本高昂，阻碍了其在实时系统中的应用。作者认为这一成本是结构性的而非根本性的，可以通过在部署流中共享信息来显著降低。

Method: 提出RRISE框架：(1) 训练一个学习代理（surrogate），以预计算的蒙特卡洛类别计数目标通过软标签交叉熵损失进行监督；(2) 通过一次性共形校准步骤将代理预测转化为可证明保守的认证半径。得到的认证是可部署验证的：当校准半径为正时，代理预测与平滑分类器一致，且平滑分类器在该半径球内是常数。

Result: 在多个图像分类基准上，RRISE的固定预算蒙特卡洛认证精度差距在0.84个百分点以内；将每次查询的10^4次噪声基模型评估替换为单次代理前向传播；约10^5次部署查询即可回收蒙特卡洛训练成本；在CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上，相较此前唯一的离线代理方法（该方法已失效），RRISE实现1.23-1.91倍更高的认证精度。

Conclusion: RRISE将高效随机平滑确立为重复部署场景中实现可证明鲁棒性的实用路径，通过信息共享显著降低认证计算成本，同时保持保守的认证保证。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing (RS) uses a smoothed classifier to provide architecture-agnostic certificates of $\ell_2$ classification robustness, but its dependence on per-input Monte Carlo (MC) sampling undermines its use in real-time systems. We argue that this cost is structural rather than fundamental, such that it can be significantly reduced by sharing information across the deployment stream. We introduce RRISE, an RS framework that compresses certification into a single forward pass through a learned surrogate. RRISE trains the surrogate against precomputed MC class-count targets via a soft-label cross-entropy loss and converts surrogate predictions into provably conservative certified radii through a one-time conformal calibration step. The resulting certificate is deployment-verifiable: whenever the calibrated radius is positive, the surrogate's prediction provably matches the smoothed classifier's and the smoothed classifier is constant on a ball of that radius around the input. Across image classification benchmarks, RRISE matches fixed-budget MC certified accuracy within $0.84$ percentage points while replacing up to $10^4$ noisy base-model evaluations per query with a single surrogate forward pass, recouping MC training cost after $\approx 10^5$ deployment queries. On CIFAR-100 and Tiny ImageNet, where the only prior offline-surrogate method collapses, RRISE achieves $1.23$ to $1.91\times$ higher certified accuracy, establishing efficient randomized smoothing as a practical path to certified robustness in repeated-deployment settings.

</details>


### [129] [Are we really tilting? The mechanics of reward guidance in flow and diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02884)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了奖励引导扩散模型中奖励黑客行为的根本原因——Doob h函数的有限粒子插件估计近似，并在高斯混合目标及FLUX.1文生图任务中验证了理论，提出了无额外计算成本的奖励阻尼方案来修正模态内偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导算法在推理时引导生成过程朝向奖励倾斜的测度，但存在奖励黑客问题（过度优化奖励而牺牲对学习分布的保真度）。前人将其归因于神经奖励函数的复杂性或扩散训练的隐式偏差，但其根本起源尚不清楚。本文旨在从理论上阐明奖励黑客的根本原因。

Method: 在高斯和高斯混合目标（二次奖励）的简单非平凡设定下，从闭式解中隔离出有限粒子插件估计Doob h函数的两个不同失败模式：模态内偏差（导致各模态内奖励黑客）和模态选择失败（无法选择高奖励模态）。提出闭式奖励阻尼调度来修正模态内偏差（无需额外计算），并阐明best-of-n采样在补偿模态选择失败中的作用。

Result: 在高斯混合目标、2D棋盘分布和FLUX.1文生图任务上的实验证实了理论见解适用于实际场景。

Conclusion: 奖励黑客的根本原因是Doob h函数的有限粒子插件估计近似，而非神经网络的复杂性或扩散训练本身的偏置。所提出的奖励阻尼调度可以有效修正模态内偏差，而best-of-n采样可弥补模态选择失败。

Abstract: Reward guidance algorithms steer a learned generative process toward the reward-tilted measure at inference time. While empirically powerful, these methods are prone to reward hacking: the guided model over-optimizes the reward at the cost of fidelity to the learned distribution. Prior work has attributed this to the complexity of neural reward functions or implicit biases in diffusion training, but its fundamental origins remain poorly understood. We show that reward hacking arises from an approximation made in most practical implementations of reward-guided diffusion -- finite-particle plug-in estimation of the Doob h-function -- even in the simplest non-trivial settings of Gaussian and Gaussian mixture targets with quadratic rewards. In closed form, we isolate two distinct failure modes of the plug-in estimator: it leads to reward hacking within each mode and it cannot select high-reward modes. We propose a closed-form reward damping schedule that corrects the within-mode bias with no additional compute, and clarify the role of best-of-n sampling in compensating for the mode selection failure. Experiments on Gaussian mixture targets, a 2D checkerboard, and FLUX.1 text-to-image generation confirm that our theoretical insights carry over to practical settings.

</details>


### [130] [Scalable Uncertainty Quantification for Extreme Weather Forecasting via Empirical Neural Tangent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02886)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Rex Gregor Laylo,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经正切核（NTK）的不确定性量化方法（NTK-UQ），利用最后一层经验特征实现天气预报的不确定性估计。理论分析揭示了UQ质量依赖于架构的两种机制：方差坍缩机制（当特征值截断秩接近特征空间有效秩时，GP修正项消耗几乎所有先验方差，导致极端天气与常规条件无法区分）和分解性能机制（ICA利用高阶统计量分离重尾极端事件特征，优于仅捕捉二阶方差的SVD）。提出基于特征谱浓度比的数据驱动选择规则。相比分割共形预测，NTK-UQ在90%覆盖下预测区间锐减31-37%，且能生成随极端事件严重程度自适应缩放的区间，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习天气模型虽速度快精度高，但生成的是确定性预测，缺乏不确定性估计，这在极端天气等高风险决策场景中是一个关键缺陷。

Method: 基于NTK的最后一层经验特征不确定性量化，结合高斯过程修正项。利用独立成分分析（ICA）和奇异值分解（SVD）进行分解，通过特征谱浓度比数据驱动选择最优分解方法。

Result: 相比分割共形预测，NTK-UQ在90%覆盖下预测区间锐减31-37%；能生成自适应区间，随极端事件严重程度缩放；仅需每个样本一次矩阵向量乘积，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: NTK-UQ为深度学习天气模型提供了高效、架构感知的不确定性量化方法，显著优于共形预测基线，尤其适用于极端天气事件的风险决策。

Abstract: Deep learning weather models now match numerical weather prediction accuracy while running orders of magnitude faster, but produce deterministic forecasts without uncertainty estimates, a critical gap for high-stakes decisions during extreme weather events. This paper proposes Neural Tangent Kernel-based uncertainty quantification (NTK-UQ) using last-layer empirical features. Theoretical analysis predicts that UQ quality is architecture-dependent through two mechanisms. First, a variance collapse mechanism explains when UQ fails: when the eigenvalue truncation rank approaches the effective rank of the feature space, the GP correction term consumes nearly all prior variance, destroying discrimination between tropical cyclones and routine conditions; architectures with concentrated spectra (spectral operators) require aggressive truncation ($k \leq 10$), while attention-based models tolerate full-rank computation. Second, decomposition performance depends on the non-Gaussian, heavy-tailed structure of extreme weather: Independent Component Analysis exploits higher-order statistics (kurtosis, negentropy) to isolate heavy-tailed extreme-event features, achieving higher discrimination than singular value decomposition, which captures only second-order variance. A data-driven selection rule chooses ICA or SVD from the feature eigenspectrum concentration ratio, correctly prescribing the superior decomposition for all four evaluated architectures. Compared to split conformal prediction (the natural post-hoc baseline), NTK-UQ achieves 31--37\% sharper prediction intervals at 90\% coverage, and uniquely produces \emph{adaptive} intervals that scale with extreme event severity, which conformal prediction cannot achieve by construction. The framework requires no retraining; inference-time uncertainty requires only a single matrix-vector product per sample.

</details>


### [131] [A Nonmonotone Gradient-Based Algorithm for Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization and Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02887)
*Ryan Swart,Johannes Brust*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法应用于对称非负矩阵分解（Symmetric NMF），提出了SNMPBB算法及其图聚类扩展Graph-SNMPBB和大规模低秩近似扩展LAI-SNMPBB，在合成数据上实现6倍加速，在真实聚类基准和SuiteSparse矩阵上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解中，投影梯度方法长期被认为收敛缓慢，需要证明梯度算法可以比以往认知更有效。

Method: 提出SNMPBB（非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法），及其两个扩展：Graph-SNMPBB（加入图拉普拉斯正则化用于图聚类）和LAI-SNMPBB（低秩近似用于大规模问题）。证明了全局收敛到一阶稳定点，以及随机近似下Barzilai-Borwein曲率信息保持性。

Result: 合成数据上SNMPBB比SymANLS快6倍，且优势随秩增加而扩大；在6个真实聚类基准中Graph-SNMPBB匹配或超越SymANLS精度；LAI-SNMPBB在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上的运行时间和残差质量均优于LAI-SymPGNCG。

Conclusion: 梯度算法在对称NMF中远比以往认知更有效，所提方法在速度、精度和可扩展性上全面领先现有方案。

Abstract: Symmetric nonnegative matrix factorization (Symmetric NMF) approximates a matrix as $WW^T$ with nonnegative rectangular factor $W$. It has broad applications in graph clustering and machine learning. In contrast to the NMF, projected gradient methods for the symmetric problem had been associated with slow convergence. To address this, we introduce SNMPBB, the first adaptation of nonmonotone projected Barzilai-Borwein methods to Symmetric NMF, demonstrating that gradient algorithms are significantly more effective than previously understood. We further extend SNMPBB to graph clustering using the graph Laplacian regularization (Graph-SNMPBB) and to large problems with low-rank approximations (LAI-SNMPBB). For all variants we prove global convergence to first-order stationary points and also that Barzilai-Borwein curvature information is preserved with randomized approximations. On synthetic data, SNMPBB achieves 6 times speedup over the alternative SymANLS for similar residuals, with advantages growing at higher ranks. Across six real-world clustering benchmarks, Graph-SNMPBB matches or exceeds SymANLS accuracy. Lastly, LAI-SNMPBB outperforms state-of-the-art LAI-SymPGNCG on 34 SuiteSparse matrices in both runtime and residual quality.

</details>


### [132] [Multi-Modal Machine Learning for Breast Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02892)
*Jiahao Shao,Xudong Wang,Anam Nawaz Khan,Christopher Brett,Xueping Li,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了结合治疗记录、病理报告和临床笔记的多模态临床数据对乳腺癌复发预测的影响，提出了一种基于规则的正则表达式提取与优先级冲突消解策略，从非结构化病理文本中恢复肿瘤特征以增强结构化记录。实验表明，多模态融合相较单模态方法持续提升了预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌复发是幸存者长期死亡的主要原因，需要及时准确的风险评估来指导随访和治疗规划。传统预测模型通常仅使用结构化或非结构化单一数据类型，难以捕获完整的临床背景信息。

Method: 采用基于规则的正则表达式提取机制，结合优先级的冲突消解策略，从自由文本病理报告中恢复确切的肿瘤特征，用于增强结构化记录；在多种机器学习模型上分别评估单源和多模态输入的性能。

Result: 多模态融合相较于单模态方法在预测准确性上表现出一致的提升。

Conclusion: 多模态临床数据整合能有效提升乳腺癌复发预测模型的性能，优于仅使用单一数据模态的方法。

Abstract: Breast cancer recurrence, a leading cause of long-term mortality among survivors, requires timely and accurate risk assessment to guide follow-up care and treatment planning. Traditional predictive models, often limited to either structured or unstructured data alone, struggle to capture the full clinical context. This study examines the impact of integrating multi-modal clinical data, including treatment records, pathology reports, and clinician notes, on recurrence prediction. By integrating a rule-based regular expression extraction mechanism with a rigorous precedence-based conflict reconciliation strategy, our approach effectively recovers definitive tumor characteristics from free-text pathology narratives to augment structured records. We also benchmark performance against commonly used feature sets from prior breast cancer studies to assess the added value of multi-modal integration. Single-source and multi-modal inputs are evaluated across a range of machine learning models. Results show that multi-modal integration consistently improves predictive accuracy compared to single-modal methods.

</details>


### [133] [Fast Unlearning at Scale via Margin Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02920)
*Federico Di Gennaro,Alexander Shevchenko,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线停止规则的高效语言模型遗忘方法MASC，通过降低被遗忘序列的logit差距，在无需下游评估和额外存储的情况下实现竞争性的遗忘-保留权衡，并观察到随着模型规模增大遗忘效果改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型遗忘方法需要固定训练预算并依赖下游验证数据选择检查点，导致计算浪费（超出遗忘-保留权衡的训练）和存储开销（额外检查点保存与重复评估），限制了可扩展性。

Method: MASC通过在线停止规则主动降低遗忘文本序列中原始预测词与替代候选词之间的logit差距，当所有遗忘序列中足够大比例的token位置平均logit差距变小后输出最终模型，无需下游评估。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE News和MUSE Books数据集上，MASC在实现竞争性遗忘-保留权衡的同时，计算成本仅为现有基线的一小部分。此外，随着模型参数量增加，MASC和SimNPO的遗忘指标保持可比，保留效用提升。

Conclusion: MASC提出了一种无需下游验证的高效遗忘方法，通过在线停止规则显著降低了计算和存储开销，且随模型规模扩大遗忘效果改善，为大规模语言模型遗忘提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Language-model unlearning updates a trained model to behave as if it had not seen selected training examples, while preserving utility and avoiding costly retraining. Existing approaches typically fine-tune the pretrained model with a fixed training budget and select the final model afterwards by evaluating several saved checkpoints on downstream validation data. Two sources of unnecessary computation limit scalability: training beyond the desired forget-retain trade-off, and checkpoint selection that requires extra storage and repeated evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce MArgin Self-Correction (MASC), an efficient unlearning method with an online stopping rule that does not require downstream evaluation. Given a text sequence to be forgotten, MASC actively reduces the logit gap between the original next token and the most likely alternatives. It outputs a final model once this gap is small on average over a sufficiently large proportion of token positions across all forget sequences. On TOFU, MUSE News, and MUSE Books, MASC achieves a competitive forget-retain trade-off at a fraction of the computational cost of existing baselines. We further observe that as we increase model size (a.k.a. number of parameters), the trade-offs improve for both MASC and SimNPO -- the forget metrics remain comparable while retain utility increases.

</details>


### [134] [Hierarchical RBF-KAN and RBF-SKAN Architectures for Multidimensional Function Approximation and Random Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02936)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了采用径向基函数作为激活函数的分层Kolmogorov-Arnold神经网络架构（hierarchical RBF-KAN和hierarchical RBF-SKAN），用于确定性函数逼近和随机场学习。理论上证明了通用逼近性质，并给出了定量逼近估计，表明该方法能部分缓解高维函数学习的维数灾难；实验验证了该结构能有效学习多元函数和随机场模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维确定性函数逼近和随机场学习中的维数灾难问题，现有的神经网络架构（如标准KAN）在处理高维问题时仍面临挑战，亟需设计更有效的网络结构来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出了两种分层架构：1) hierarchical RBF-KAN，采用径向基函数作为激活函数进行确定性函数逼近；2) hierarchical RBF-SKAN，用于随机场学习。从理论上证明了通用逼近性质，推导了定量逼近估计，并在Wasserstein-2度量下证明了随机场逼近能力。

Result: 理论上证明了两种架构的通用逼近性质；定量估计表明该方法可通过降低逼近问题的有效维度来部分缓解维数灾难；实验验证了该结构能有效学习多元函数和随机场模型。

Conclusion: 基于径向基函数的分层KAN架构在理论上具有缓解维数灾难的潜力，在实践中能有效逼近高维函数和随机场，为高维近似问题提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we propose and analyze hierarchical Kolmogorov--Arnold neural network architectures employing radial basis functions as activation functions for approximating deterministic functions and random field models. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical radial-basis-function Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-KAN) for multidimensional deterministic function approximation and a hierarchical radial-basis-function stochastic Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-SKAN) for random field learning. From a theoretical perspective, we establish universal approximation results for both architectures. In particular, we derive quantitative approximation estimates for the hierarchical RBF-KAN, showing that the proposed framework has the potential to partially alleviate the curse of dimensionality in learning high-dimensional functions by reducing the effective dimensionality of the approximation problem. Furthermore, we show that the hierarchical RBF-SKAN can approximate random field models under the Wasserstein-2 metric. Empirically, we show that our proposed radial-basis-function-based neural network structure could effectively learn multivariate functions and random field models.

</details>


### [135] [ERP-XTTN: Interpretable Prototype-Guided Cross-Attention for Cross-Subject ERP Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02939)
*Charlotte Genevier Wyman,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERP-XTTN，一种基于原型交叉注意力的可解释脑机接口分类器，在无需校准的跨被试泛化条件下，以极小的性能代价（3通道AUROC差距0.018，全导联0.034）实现与EEGNet和xDAWN+RG相竞争的ERP分类性能，并在三个公开数据集八个ERP组件上进行了LOSO评估，首次在ERP CORE上建立epoch级LOSO基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释BCI分类器难以在无校准条件下实现跨被试泛化，且黑盒模型无法揭示跨被试信号结构；需要一种部署兼容、无需校准且具备内在可解释性的分类方法。

Method: 提出ERP-XTTN交叉注意力架构，将输入的EEG patches通过仅有查询-键的交叉注意力路由至固定差分波原型（无值投影），分类完全依赖注意力路由，注意力忠实性为结构性而非事后解释；原型从训练折差分波的极值自动导出。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（BNCI、HRI Cursor、ERP CORE）八个ERP组件（ERN、LRP、ErrP、N170、P300、N2pc、MMN、N400）上，LOSO评估下3通道AUROC差距0.018，全导联0.034；误差来源为时间灵活性成本（相对于EEGNet）和空间利用成本（相对于xDAWN+RG，受信噪比驱动）。

Conclusion: ERP-XTTN在因果、无校准条件下实现对多样ERP组件的跨被试泛化，以极小可解释性代价获得竞争性能；透明路由揭示了黑盒模型无法发现的跨被试信号结构（假阳性比真阴性更接近真阳性），表明分类错误具有神经生理学可解释性。

Abstract: Interpretable brain-computer interface classifiers that generalize across subjects without calibration remain an open challenge. We test whether prototype-based cross-attention can provide competitive, interpretable event-related potential (ERP) classification under deployment-compatible conditions. We propose ERP-XTTN, a cross-attention architecture that routes input EEG patches to fixed difference-wave prototypes via query-key-only cross-attention with no value projection, so classification depends entirely on attention routing and attention faithfulness is structural rather than post-hoc. Prototypes are derived automatically from extrema in the training-fold difference wave. We evaluate across three public sources (BNCI Horizon 2020, HRI Cursor, and ERP CORE) spanning eight ERP components (ERN, LRP, ErrP, N170, P300, N2pc, MMN, N400), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation with causal filtering at two channel counts (3-channel and full montage), against EEGNet and xDAWN with Riemannian geometry (xDAWN+RG). The mean gap between the best baseline and ERP-XTTN was .018 AUROC at 3 channels and .034 at full montage, arising from two largely distinct sources: a temporal-flexibility cost relative to EEGNet and a spatial-exploitation cost relative to xDAWN+RG, the latter driven by signal-to-noise ratio at full montage. Beyond accuracy, the transparent routing reveals cross-subject signal structure that black-box models cannot: false positives resembled true positives more than true negatives did, indicating that classification errors are neurophysiologically explicable. ERP-XTTN generalizes across diverse ERPs under causal, calibration-free conditions with a small interpretability cost at minimal montages. To our knowledge, this is the first epoch-level LOSO benchmark on ERP CORE.

</details>


### [136] [Outsmarting the Chameleon: Counterfactual Decoupling for Tactical OOD Shifts in Live Streaming Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02946)
*Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Jiaqi Xu,Yang Liu,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LPCD的潜变量预测反事实解耦框架，用于解决直播流中因恶意意图不断重构叙事包装而产生的战术性分布外偏移问题，通过潜层反事实一致性约束和轻量级推理时校准，在工业级数据集和在线流量上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播流中恶意行为者保持稳定目标但不断重构叙事包装以逃避检测，这种战术性分布外偏移使得现有OOD泛化范式的假设（如意图-策略解耦与原始层面反事实）难以满足，亟需一种能处理意图与叙事紧密耦合演进的新方法。

Method: 从潜层因果视角出发，提出LPCD框架：在潜变量层面建模意图与叙事变化，施加潜层反事实一致性约束以锚定因果稳定的恶意意图；推理时采用轻量级、无参数的校准方法进一步缓解策略引发的分布偏移。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集和在线生产流量上的大量实验表明，LPCD持续优于现有最先进基线方法，验证了其在真实直播场景中缓解演化的对抗性风险的有效性。

Conclusion: LPCD通过潜层因果反事实推理有效应对了直播流中战术性OOD偏移问题，为鲁棒的直播风险评估提供了可插拔的解决方案。

Abstract: Live streaming has emerged as a primary medium for social interaction and digital commerce, yet it is increasingly plagued by sophisticated risks. A fundamental challenge in this domain is \emph{tactical out-of-distribution (OOD) shift}: while malicious actors maintain stable underlying objectives, they continuously redesign narrative packaging to evade detection. Such adversarial shifts expose critical limitations of existing OOD generalization paradigms, whose assumptions are difficult to satisfy in the presence of tightly coupled intent-tactic evolution and ill-defined raw-level counterfactuals.
  In this paper, we tackle this issue from a \emph{latent causal} perspective and propose \underline{L}atent-\underline{P}redictive \underline{C}ounterfactual \underline{D}ecoupling~(LPCD), a plug-in framework for robust live streaming risk assessment. LPCD enables counterfactual reasoning under adversarial tactical re-packaging by modeling intent and narrative variation at the latent level, and enforces \emph{latent counterfactual consistency} to anchor risk prediction on causally stable malicious intent. At inference time, LPCD applies a lightweight, parameter-free calibration to further mitigate tactic-induced distribution shifts. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets and online production traffic demonstrate that LPCD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating its effectiveness in moderating evolving adversarial risks in real-world live streaming. The project page is available at https://qiaoyran.github.io/LiveStreamingRiskAssessment/.

</details>


### [137] [BYORn: Bootstrap Your Own Responses to Defend Large Vision-Language Models Against Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02947)
*Ivan Sabolić,Marin Oršić,Josip Šarić,Sven Lončarić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BYORn是一种针对视觉语言模型后门攻击的鲁棒微调框架，通过检测并替换语义不匹配的毒化目标响应，打破触发器与目标输出的关联，从而提升模型鲁棒性，同时保持干净任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调范式在视觉语言模型的后门攻击下高度脆弱，且现有防御在开放文本生成场景中效果不佳。

Method: BYORn框架基于毒化目标响应通常语义不合理的观察，识别这些不对齐的响应并动态替换为模型自身生成的替代响应，从而打破触发器与目标输出的相关性。其梯度对应于干净数据分布上总体风险上界的经验估计梯度。

Result: BYORn持续提升对后门攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持干净任务性能，建立了泛化性能与攻击成功率之间新的权衡边界，并且对自适应攻击依然有效。

Conclusion: BYORn是一种有效的后门鲁棒微调框架，在保持下游任务性能的同时显著提升防御能力，并具备对自适应攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning is the predominant approach for adapting autoregressive vision-language models to downstream tasks. Recent work has shown that this paradigm is highly vulnerable to backdoor attacks, and that existing defenses are ineffective in open-ended generation settings. In response, we propose BYORn, a backdoor-robust fine-tuning framework motivated by the observation that poisoned target responses are often semantically implausible given the corresponding image-text inputs and a pretrained model. BYORn identifies such misaligned responses and dynamically replaces them with alternative responses generated by the model, thereby breaking the correlation between triggers and target outputs. The resulting objective gradient corresponds to the gradient of the empirical estimate of the population risk upper bound over the clean data distribution. Empirically, BYORn consistently improves robustness to backdoor attacks while preserving clean-task performance, establishing a new trade-off frontier between generalization and attack success rate. Finally, we demonstrate that BYORn remains effective against adaptive attacks specifically designed to circumvent the proposed defense.

</details>


### [138] [From Non-Convex to Strongly Convex: Curvature-Adaptive FTPL for Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02948)
*Moses Charikar,Chirag Pabbaraju,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在非凸在线优化中能够自适应曲率的Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL)算法，通过时变扰动尺度（基于跟随者领者的调参规则）实现：在一般非凸Lipschitz损失下达到O(√T)遗憾，在累积曲率线性增长（含强凸情形）时达到O(log T)遗憾，并证明了该权衡是本质性的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FTPL在非凸损失下虽能达到最优O(√T)遗憾，但无法利用曲率信息实现更快的收敛速率。希望在非凸设定下实现曲率自适应性，无需预先知道曲率的累积方式。

Method: 将标准FTPL的固定扰动尺度替换为仅利用过去信息的时变尺度，并提出一个简单的跟随者领者（follow-the-leader）调参规则来动态选择该尺度。

Result: 算法在任意非凸Lipschitz损失下达到O(√T)遗憾；当累积曲率线性增长（含强凸情形）时，在足够精确的预言机调用下达到O(log T)遗憾。同时给出了匹配的下界，证明该权衡是本质的。

Conclusion: FTPL可以扩展到非凸设定下的曲率自适应，且最坏情况非凸遗憾与曲率驱动快速率之间的权衡是内在的、不可改进的。

Abstract: Curvature adaptivity is a classical theme in online optimization: for convex Lipschitz losses, adaptive methods interpolate between the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for general convex losses and $O(\log T)$ regret under strong convexity. Recent work has shown that Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) achieves optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret even for online non-convex Lipschitz losses, assuming access to an approximate offline-optimization oracle, but these guarantees do not exploit curvature. We show that FTPL can be made curvature-adaptive in the non-convex setting, without knowing in advance how curvature will accumulate over time. Our algorithm replaces the fixed perturbation scale of standard FTPL with a time-varying scale chosen using only past information. We give a simple follow-the-leader tuning rule for this scale and show that it competes, up to constants, with the best choice in hindsight. The resulting method achieves $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for arbitrary non-convex Lipschitz losses and improves as cumulative curvature grows; with sufficiently accurate oracle calls, it achieves $O(\log T)$ regret when cumulative curvature grows linearly, which includes the classical strongly convex regime. We complement these upper bounds with matching lower bounds for prescribed cumulative-curvature sequences, already for one-dimensional convex losses, showing that the tradeoff between worst-case non-convex regret and curvature-driven fast rates is intrinsic.

</details>


### [139] [Gate AI: LLM Security Benchmark Evaluation Methodology and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02959)
*Ryle Goehausen,Marcus Sousa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套评估LLM提示注入和越狱检测器的标准化测试框架，解决了现有评估中每数据集阈值调优和操作点未公开两个系统性问题，通过全局阈值、16个基准测试和多种诊断方法实现更公平、可复现的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM提示注入和越狱检测器的评估存在两个系统性弱点：一是针对每个数据集单独调优阈值，导致结果泛化性差；二是操作点（operating points）未公开，使得结果无法复现和公平比较。需要一套标准化的评估框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 评估框架在16个公开基准测试（共12,111个样本）上使用5折交叉验证。主流程为按行StratifiedKFold，并行使用StratifiedGroupKFold（基于父提示ID和MinHash+LSH近重复聚类（Jaccard≥0.8））作为泄漏诊断。在保留折上选择单一全局操作点（最大F1且FPR≤1%），统一应用于所有数据集。通过留一数据集交叉验证、随机标签对照、对抗验证、排列特征重要性、长度偏倚相关性、分类器头一致性、跨源近重复检测、阈值可迁移性、训练集与OOF一致性、释义不变性探测等多项诊断测试泛化能力。外部比较时，将检测器阈值重新调整到竞争者的公布FPR上。

Result: 提出了一个更严谨、可复现的评估框架，避免了每数据集阈值调优的过拟合问题，并通过多维度诊断测试系统性地评估检测器的泛化能力。外部比较在匹配的操作点上进行，确保公平对比。

Conclusion: 该评估框架通过全局阈值、交叉验证和多维度诊断，为LLM提示注入和越狱检测器的评估提供了标准化方案，解决了现有评估中的关键方法论缺陷，有助于推动该领域更可靠的研究进展。

Abstract: Published evaluations of prompt-injection and jailbreak detectors for Large Language Models often suffer from two systematic weaknesses: per-dataset threshold tuning and undisclosed operating points. We describe an evaluation harness that addresses both. The detector under evaluation is scored across 16 public benchmarks (12,111 samples) using 5-fold cross-validation. StratifiedKFold (by row) is the headline pass; a parallel StratifiedGroupKFold pass over a composite key (parent-prompt id plus MinHash + LSH near-duplicate clusters at Jaccard $\gtrsim 0.8$) runs alongside it as a leakage-premium diagnostic. A single global operating point is selected on the held-out folds (max F1 subject to FPR $\leq 1\%$) and applied uniformly to every dataset, so per-dataset results reflect one threshold rather than per-benchmark optimisation. Generalisation is examined through a battery of diagnostics (leave-one-dataset-out cross-validation, a random-label control, adversarial validation, permutation feature importance, length-bias correlation, classifier-head agreement, cross-source near-duplicate detection, threshold transferability, train-vs-OOF agreement, and a paraphrase-invariance probe), most with a quantitative pass threshold and the remainder with a stated failure mode. For every external comparison, the detector's threshold is re-tuned to the competitor's published false-positive rate so head-to-head values are evaluated at matched operating points.

</details>


### [140] [KForge: LLM-Driven Cross-Platform Kernel Generation for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02963)
*Taras Sereda,Burak Bartan,Ankita Nayak,Tom St. John,Natalie Serrino,Zain Asgar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KForge是一个跨平台框架，利用两个LLM代理（生成代理和性能分析代理）的协作迭代循环，自动为异构加速器生成高性能内核。在NVIDIA B200上相比TensorRT-LLM提升2.12%吞吐量，在Intel Arc B580上相比PyTorch实现5.13倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推理场景日益面向异构混合加速器，而不同计算阶段（推理、工具调用、多智能体协调）具有不同的计算和内存特征，需要为不同硬件后端手动编写高性能内核，但手工编写耗时且无法扩展。

Method: 提出KForge框架，包含两个LLM驱动的智能体：（1）生成智能体：生成并逐步精炼内核，利用编译和正确性反馈；（2）性能分析智能体：解释性能分析数据（从编程API到GUI工具）并发出优化建议。流程交替进行功能通过（确保正确性）和优化通过（缩小与手工调优基线的差距）。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200上，相比TensorRT-LLM在gpt-oss-20b推理速度基准上实现2.12%的端到端吞吐量提升。在Intel Arc B580上，针对KernelBench Level 2的37个GEMM+尾部操作负载，生成Triton内核实现相比PyTorch eager和torch.compile中较优者的5.13倍几何平均加速，主要得益于算子融合和混合精度执行。

Conclusion: KForge展示了基于LLM代理协作的迭代精炼框架能够有效自动化异构加速器上的内核生成，在两种截然不同的硬件后端上均超越了手工优化的基线，证明了该方法在跨平台内核生成中的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: Production inference increasingly targets a heterogeneous mix of accelerators. Agentic pipelines interleave reasoning, tool calls, and multi-agent coordination, each with distinct compute and memory profiles. For optimal efficiency, each stage should run on the accelerator best suited to it. This creates a systems challenge: each pipeline now requires high-performance kernels across a growing set of hardware backends and programming models. Writing these kernels by hand is time-consuming, demands deep low-level expertise, and does not scale as kernel complexity grows. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been leveraged for automatic kernel generation, but challenges in low-level code generation and cross-backend generalization persist. We present KForge, a cross-platform framework built around an iterative refinement loop driven by two collaborating LLM-based agents: a generation agent that produces and progressively refines kernels using compilation and correctness feedback, and a performance-analysis agent that interprets profiling data, from programmatic APIs to GUI-based tools, and emits recommendations that steer the next round of synthesis. The loop alternates between functional passes, which drive a candidate to correctness, and optimization passes, which close the performance gap to hand-tuned baselines. We evaluate KForge on two backends with very different baseline reference availability. On NVIDIA B200, KForge achieves a 2.12$\%$ improvement in end-to-end throughput compared to TensorRT-LLM on the gpt-oss-20b inference speed benchmark. On Intel Arc B580, KForge generates Triton kernels achieving a 5.13$\times$ geometric mean speedup over the faster of PyTorch eager and torch.compile on 37 GEMM + tail-ops workloads from KernelBench Level 2, primarily via operator fusion and mixed-precision execution.

</details>


### [141] [Neural Networks Provably Learn Spectral Representations for Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02993)
*Jianliang He,Leda Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Siyu Chen,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将投影梯度流提升到傅里叶域，揭示了双层神经网络在群组合任务中的训练动力学。研究表明，在随机初始化下，每个神经元几乎必然收敛到单个不可约表示，交叉层傅里叶系数达到旋转秩一对齐，从而提供了特征学习的表示理论解释，并刻画了矩阵值群表示的低秩压缩现象。对于阿贝尔群，随机初始化促进不可约表示间均匀多样化并诱导哈尔均匀相位，通过多数投票机制联合逼近指标函数，相位对齐和表示竞争以指数收敛速率出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络训练中结构化内部表征如何涌现是深度学习研究的核心问题。本文通过群组合任务（预测有限群中两个元素的乘积）来研究该现象，旨在从表示理论角度为特征学习和低秩压缩提供严格的理论解释。

Method: 将投影梯度流提升到傅里叶域，证明训练动力学由表示论能量泛函上的黎曼梯度上升控制；利用随机初始化条件下的几乎必然收敛分析，以及阿贝尔群情形下的均匀多样化与哈尔均匀相位机理。

Result: 证明每个神经元几乎必然收敛到单个不可约表示，交叉层傅里叶系数实现旋转秩一对齐；对阿贝尔群，随机初始化导致表示均匀多样化和哈尔均匀相位，通过多数投票近似指标函数；相位对齐和表示竞争以指数速率收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经网络中的特征学习提供了表示理论框架，揭示了矩阵值群表示的低秩压缩现象，并为理解结构化表征的涌现提供了严格的动力学分析。

Abstract: Understanding how structured internal structure emerges during neural network training is central to the study of deep learning. We investigate this phenomenon through the group composition task, where a two-layer neural network is trained to predict $g_1 \star g_2$ for elements of a finite group $G$. By lifting the projected gradient flow to the Fourier domain, we demonstrate that the training dynamics are governed by a Riemannian gradient ascent on a representation-theoretic energy functional. We prove that, under random initialization, this flow drives each neuron to converge almost surely toward a single irreducible representation, while the cross-layer Fourier coefficients achieve a rotational rank-one alignment. This framework provides a representation-theoretic account of feature learning and characterizes a novel low-rank compression phenomenon for matrix-valued group representations. Moreover, for Abelian groups, we provide a complete population-level description: random initialization promotes uniform diversification across nontrivial representations and induces Haar-uniform phases, jointly approximating the indicator via a majority-vote mechanism. We further prove that both phase alignment and representation competition emerge with exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [142] [CoughSense: Five-Class Respiratory Disease Classification via Whisper Encoder Fine-Tuning and Dual-Encoder Cross-Attention Fusion with Balanced Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02998)
*Nikhil Vincent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoughSense 是一个基于智能手机咳嗽录音的五分类呼吸疾病诊断系统（健康、COVID-19、哮喘、支气管炎、肺炎），使用 Whisper 编码器作为骨干网络，通过主动帧 QKV 注意力池化解决静音稀释问题，结合多种训练策略处理类别不平衡和跨数据集域偏移，在五个公开数据集共18,301条录音上达到82.3%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动咳嗽分析大多局限于二元 COVID-19 检测，缺乏能够区分多种呼吸系统疾病的实用工具。研究者希望利用消费级智能手机实现低成本、多类别的呼吸系统疾病筛查。

Method: 1) 使用 OpenAI Whisper 编码器作为预训练骨干网络；2) 提出主动帧 QKV 注意力池化（Active-frame QKV attention pooling），将注意力限制在前200个编码器token上，解决 Whisper 30秒窗口中的静音稀释问题；3) 采用 WeightedRandomSampler、SpecAugment、Balanced Mixup、监督对比辅助损失、FiLM 症状条件化、梯度反转域适应等策略处理类别不平衡和域偏移；4) 双编码器模型融合 Whisper 与 OPERA-CT 呼吸基础模型（通过交叉注意力）。

Result: CoughSense（Whisper-tiny，8.6M参数）在五折交叉验证上达到 82.3% 平衡准确率，宏F1为0.817，AUC为0.941。优于 ImageNet 预训练的 EfficientNet-B2 11.1个点，优于从头训练的 ViT 29.6个点。所有五类召回率超过74%，四类超过80%。双编码器模型达到85.4%平衡准确率。主动帧池化贡献最大（5.1个点）。

Conclusion: CoughSense 证明了利用预训练语音模型（Whisper）进行多类别咳嗽疾病分类的可行性，主动帧注意力池化有效解决了短音频任务中的静音稀释问题，可作为任何使用 Whisper 骨干网络的短音频任务的通用改进方向。

Abstract: Automated cough analysis offers a path to low-cost respiratory screening, but most existing work stops at binary COVID-19 detection. A practical tool needs to tell apart several respiratory conditions from one cough recording on a consumer smartphone. We present CoughSense, a system that sorts cough recordings into five classes. These are healthy, COVID-19, asthma or respiratory condition, bronchitis, and pneumonia. We aggregated 18,301 recordings from four public datasets (Coswara, CoughVID, Virufy, and the West China Hospital Pediatric Cough Dataset) and used the OpenAI Whisper encoder as a pretrained backbone for cough disease classification. The main contribution is active-frame QKV attention pooling, which restricts attention to the first 200 of 1500 encoder tokens. This avoids the silence-dilution problem that arises because a 3-second cough fills only 150 tokens of Whisper's 30-second input window. Other training parts handle the 19 to 1 class imbalance and the four-dataset domain shift. These include WeightedRandomSampler, SpecAugment, Balanced Mixup with forced minority pairing, a supervised contrastive auxiliary loss, FiLM symptom conditioning, and gradient-reversal domain adaptation. A dual-encoder model fuses Whisper with the OPERA-CT respiratory foundation model through cross-attention. CoughSense (Whisper-tiny, 8.6M parameters) reached 82.3 percent balanced accuracy on five-fold cross-validation (macro-F1 of 0.817, AUC of 0.941). It beat an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B2 by 11.1 points and a ViT trained from scratch by 29.6 points. All five classes passed 74 percent recall and four of five passed 80 percent. The dual-encoder model reached 85.4 percent balanced accuracy. Active-frame pooling is the largest single contributor across all ablation components at 5.1 points, which should help any short-audio task using Whisper as a backbone.

</details>


### [143] [How Quantization Changes Interpretable Features: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量化后的语言模型中，从全精度模型提取的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征是否仍然保持可靠。通过固定SAE作为测量基准，比较INT8至INT4位宽下全精度与RTN量化激活的Pearson相关性，发现特征退化是渐进式的，而非全部同时失效。特征存活率可从全精度统计量预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），且任务指标（如困惑度）可能掩盖特征层面的损伤。量化和幅度剪枝对特征集的破坏高度重叠，表明存在共享的压缩诱导脆弱性。结论：行为层面的等价性不足以证明可解释性发现可迁移至量化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量化模型在困惑度或下游准确率上接近全精度模型，但安全审计和干预调控日益依赖可解释特征，而量化后这些特征是否仍然存在和可靠尚未被验证。

Method: 使用冻结的SAE作为固定测量基，在相同tokens上编码全精度和RTN量化激活，通过Pearson相关系数量化每个特征在量化前后的存活程度，扫描INT8至INT4位宽，在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B上进行实验。

Result: 特征存活是渐进的：INT6下Pythia-70M有62.4%特征存活，Gemma-2-2M有51.3%；存活率可由全精度统计量预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），峰值激活是最强预测因子；INT7在Gemma-2-2B上改善困惑度但同时退化18.7%特征；量化与幅度剪枝对特征集的损伤高度重叠（Jaccard 0.79-0.86，损伤评分Spearman 0.98）。

Conclusion: 行为层面的等价性（如困惑度）不足以证明可解释性发现可迁移至量化部署，需要进行特征级别的压缩审计。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and a quantized model is typically judged acceptable when its perplexity or downstream accuracy stays close to the full-precision original. Whether the model still computes in the same way, or whether the interpretable features identified in the full-precision model survive weight rounding, is rarely tested, even as safety audits and steering interventions increasingly rely on those features. We ask whether sparse autoencoder (SAE) features extracted from a dense full-precision model remain faithful once that model is quantized. Using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, we encode full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens and quantify per-feature survival by Pearson correlation, sweeping bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. We find that feature survival is graded: features degrade systematically rather than failing all at once, with 62.4 percent of active features surviving at INT6 on Pythia-70M and 51.3 percent surviving at INT6 on Gemma-2-2B, and with most non-survivors blurred rather than destroyed. Survival is predictable from full-precision statistics alone, with cross-validated AUCs of 0.92 to 0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Critically, task metrics can miss this damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7 percent of features. Finally, quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap of 0.79 to 0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation of 0.98, suggesting a shared mode of compression-induced vulnerability. These results show that behavioral parity is insufficient evidence that interpretability findings transfer to quantized deployments, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [144] [Exact equivariance, kept through training, buys zero-shot generalisation across the symmetry group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03003)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，由等变编码器E和等变预测器f构建的隐空间世界模型，其训练损失具有可证明的对称性：当世界动力学携带群G通过正交表示ρ(g)作用于隐变量时，一步预测relMSE在整个群上精确不变。通过拟合一个方向上的动力学即可数学上确定整个轨道上的动力学（举一反三）。实验在笔记本电脑级别（CPU/MPS）端到端验证，等变模型比非等变基线小4.5-7.4倍，但跨分布误差低13.8-157倍。闭环控制误差在整个群上保持浮点级精确（2D/SO(2)）或统计平坦（3D/SE(3)）。对比增强、暴力缩放和软等变均无法达到浮点级精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有对称性保证的世界模型，使模型能利用系统内在的对称性（如旋转、平移）进行学习，从而在更少数据、更小模型下获得更好的泛化能力，尤其是跨分布（out-of-distribution）的泛化。

Method: 使用等变编码器E和等变预测器f构建隐空间世界模型，利用群G通过正交表示ρ(g)作用于隐变量。采用组合编码-预测残差结构，使用Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg优化。在2D/3D场景下与非等变基线进行对比，并在PushT环境下测试闭环控制。

Result: 等变模型训练后残差约10^-6，一步误差在群上平坦到五位小数（×1.00），而非等变基线跨分布误差放大13.8倍（2D）、17.2倍（3D）、157倍（SE(3)）。等变模型小4.5-7.4倍。闭环控制误差在2D/SO(2)上浮点精确，3D/SE(3)上统计平坦（95% CI不重叠）。H步展开保持平坦（×1.00，≤2×10^-7），基线残差随H复合增长。

Conclusion: 等变性为世界模型提供了根本性的对称性保证，使模型能够从有限方向中学到整个轨道上的动力学（举一反三），并且这种保证是刚性的——数据增强、暴力缩放和软等变都无法达到同样的浮点级精确度。

Abstract: A latent world model built from an equivariant encoder $E$ and an equivariant predictor $f$ inherits a provable symmetry of its training loss: when the world's dynamics genuinely carries a group $G$ acting on latents by an orthogonal representation $ρ(g)$, the one-step prediction relMSE is exactly invariant across the whole group, so fitting the dynamics on a restricted slice of orientations mathematically determines it on the entire orbit (jǔ yī fǎn sān). We verify this end-to-end at laptop scale (CPU/MPS, fully seeded). [A] The symmetry survives a real Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg run -- composed encode-then-predict residual $\sim 10^{-6}$ after optimisation, not just at initialisation, and under any optimiser. [B] One-step error is flat to five digits across the group, while a same-hypothesis-class non-equivariant baseline fits the slice but breaks out-of-distribution (VN $\times 1.00$ vs baseline $\times 13.8$ in 2D, $\times 17.2$ in 3D, $\times 157$ over the full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ladder), with the equivariant model $4.5$-$7.4\times$ smaller. [C] The same isometry argument lifts to closed loop: under a matching equivariant planner the control trajectory at orientation $g$ is exactly $ρ(g)$ applied to the seen one, so closed-loop error is invariant across the group -- float-floor-exact in 2D/$\mathrm{SO}(2)$ on real PushT and statistically flat in 3D/$\mathrm{SE}(3)$ (disjoint 95% CIs). We stress-test the prior against Sutton's Bitter Lesson: augmentation, brute-force scale, and soft-equivariance each close at most the across-group task metric, never the float-floor exactness. Because equivariance is closed under composition, the $H$-fold rollout stays flat ($\times 1.00$, $\le 2\times 10^{-7}$) at every horizon, while the baseline's residual compounds with $H$. Out of scope: task-success sweeps, planner-free invariance, and scaling.

</details>


### [145] [MOSAIC: Efficient Mixture-of-Agent Scheduling via Adaptive Aggregation and Inference Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03014)
*Saptarshi Mitra,Yifan Zhang,Rachid Karami,Phyo Pyae Moe Aung,Nazmul Takbir,Sreetama Sarkar,Souvik Kundu,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个加速混合专家系统（MoA）工作负载的调度框架，通过ILP优化专家放置和任务分配，以及置信度感知的自适应聚合，在4-GPU系统上实现最高2.5倍专家阶段、4.23倍聚合阶段和1.7~2.3倍端到端加速，同时精度损失在0.1个百分点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoA系统在有限GPU资源上执行时存在瓶颈：基于技能的专家路由导致负载不均，结合指令微调模型与长推理模型造成生成长度极端差异，传统调度策略因此出现GPU空转和吞吐量崩溃。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于整数线性规划(ILP)的调度器，利用离线剖析成本联合优化专家放置和每工作器的提示分配，复制推理专家并固定轻量专家；(2) 置信度感知的自适应聚合，利用专家间一致性跳过重型聚合器LLM处理共识查询。

Result: 在4-GPU系统上，MOSAIC相较基线调度器实现专家阶段最高2.5倍加速、聚合阶段最高4.23倍加速、端到端1.7~2.3倍加速，精度匹配在0.1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过联合优化调度与自适应聚合，有效解决了MoA系统中的负载不均问题，在不损失精度的前提下显著提升吞吐量和GPU利用率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) systems improve reasoning accuracy by routing each query to multiple expert LLMs and aggregating their outputs. Efficiently executing this workload on limited GPU resources has bottlenecks. Skill-based routing creates skewed expert demand, and combining instruction-tuned LLMs with long-reasoning models results in extreme variability in generation lengths. Consequently, traditional scheduling strategies suffer from significant GPU idling and throughput collapse due to load imbalances. We present MOSAIC, a scheduling framework to accelerate MoA workloads. First, we formulate an Integer Linear Program (ILP) based scheduler that jointly optimizes expert placement and per-worker prompt assignment from offline-profiled costs, replicating reasoning experts across workers while pinning lightweight ones. Second, MOSAIC uses confidence-aware adaptive aggregation, leveraging inter-expert agreement to bypass the heavy final aggregator LLM for consensus queries. In our 4-GPU system, MOSAIC achieves up to 2.5x expert-stage, 4.23x aggregator-stage and 1.7~2.3x end-to-end speedups over the baseline scheduler, while matching accuracy within 0.1pp.

</details>


### [146] [ConTraIRL: Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03017)
*Yikang Gui,Bikramjit Banerjee,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTraIRL框架，通过解耦学习环境动态和任务目标的潜在表征，实现逆强化学习中奖励函数的组合式迁移，在连续控制任务中实现少样本迁移到未见过的动态-目标组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IRL中的奖励迁移在面对未见过的环境动态与任务目标组合时不可靠，缺乏对这两个因素的解耦表征能力。

Method: 采用双编码器架构，分别映射到动态潜在空间和目标潜在空间；通过双重对比学习目标训练：时间对齐使动态编码器学习目标不变结构，目标编码器学习动态不变特征。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，对未见过的动态-目标配对实现有效的少样本迁移，相较于基线方法提升了样本效率和奖励恢复效果。

Conclusion: 因子化的对比抽象方法能够实现组合式奖励迁移，支持在动态与目标重新组合的场景下进行奖励推断。

Abstract: Reward transfer in Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) is unreliable when policies must generalize to unseen combinations of environment dynamics and task goals. We propose Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL (ConTraIRL), a framework that enables compositional reward transfer by learning decoupled latent representations of these two factors. ConTraIRL uses a dual-encoder architecture that maps observations into separate dynamics and goal latent spaces, trained with a dual contrastive objective. Temporal alignment encourages the dynamics encoder to learn goal-invariant structure, while the goal encoder captures dynamics-invariant features. This factorization supports reward inference under recombined dynamics-goal settings. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate effective few-shot transfer to unseen dynamics-goal pairings, improving sample efficiency and reward recovery over transfer IRL baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Will Accurate Fields Mislead Photonic Design? FromGlobal Accuracy to Port Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03038)
*Yitian Zhang,Yonghong chen,Youming Chen,Yiyang Li,Xing Zhe,Renhe Lu,Shaolin Liao,Yuzhe Ma,Zhong Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PaNO（传播对齐神经算子）和PaNO-R2（输出感知反馈变体），用于光子设计中的神经场代理建模。针对传统全局场误差指标无法准确反映局部输出端口性能的问题，通过场/媒介/读出的视角分离误差来源，在设计相关的端口功率、分束、相位等指标上显著优于现有方法NeurOLight，端口功率误差从0.2018降至0.0739，提升72.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场代理在全局场误差上表现准确，但在基于局部输出端口读出的设备排序任务中可能失效（尤其是在传播主导的MMI分束器/耦合器中），因为端口功率、分束比、相位等关键设计指标由累积模态干涉和输出窗口聚合决定，而非平均场相似度。现有方法缺乏对齐这种物理传播链路的机制。

Method: 提出场/媒介/读出（Field/Mediator/Readout）视角，将稠密复场误差与传播分布误差和输出窗口误差分离。PaNO保持全场预测接口，将隐状态组织为局部边界结构、横向模态内容、轴向传播和跨模态交互。PaNO-R2进一步引入输出感知反馈，针对端口附近的残差场分量进行优化。

Result: 在15波长可调3×3 MMI基准（4608个留出场）上，PaNO将端口功率误差从0.2018降至0.0739，虽cMAE略高；PaNO-R2在cMAE、传播分布误差、输出分布误差和端口功率误差上均达最优，端口功率和输出分布误差分别降低72.7%和72.5%。

Conclusion: 全局场精度不足以保证设计相关的读出保真度；通过对齐物理传播链路的代理设计（PaNO/PaNO-R2）能显著提升关键端口指标的预测准确性，为光子设计自动化提供了更可靠的神经代理方案。

Abstract: Neural field surrogates can accelerate photonic design loops, but a surrogate that looks accurate in global field error can still mis-rank candidate devices when the final decision depends on localized output-port readouts. This risk is acute in propagation-dominated MMI splitters and couplers, where port power, splitting, phase, and coupling are determined by accumulated modal interference and output-window aggregation rather than by average field similarity alone. We study this field-to-design mismatch through a Field/Mediator/Readout view that separates dense complex-field error from propagation-profile and output-window errors before port aggregation. To align the surrogate with this chain, we propose PaNO, a propagation-aligned neural operator that keeps the full-field prediction interface while organizing latent states around local boundary structure, transverse modal content, axial propagation, and cross-mode interaction. We also evaluate PaNO-R2, an output-aware feedback variant for residual field components near the port region. On a 15-wavelength tunable $3{\times}3$ MMI benchmark with 4608 held-out fields, PaNO lowers NeurOLight's port-power error from 0.2018 to 0.0739 despite slightly higher cMAE, showing that global field accuracy alone is not sufficient for design-relevant readout fidelity. PaNO-R2 attains the best cMAE, propagation-profile error, output-profile error, and port-power error, reducing NeurOLight's port-power and output-profile errors by 72.7\% and 72.5\%.

</details>


### [148] [What Do Students Learn? A Feature-Level Analysis of Dark Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03052)
*Seungu Kang,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Confusion Distillation（CD），一种无教师的自蒸馏方法，利用模型自身的混淆矩阵作为动态软目标。通过Interaction Tensor框架分析发现，知识蒸馏通过修剪低频、样本特定特征，鼓励学生模型依赖紧凑的可重用特征。CD在CIFAR-100上ResNet-34/50上优于CS-KD和PS-KD等自蒸馏方法1.2%，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏虽广泛应用于模型压缩，但学生模型获取特征表示的具体机制尚未被充分探索。现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖教师网络，要么性能有限。
