Experiments on the damped spring oscillator, the Lotka-Volterra system, and the advection-diffusion PDE with misspecified physics demonstrate improved parameter recovery and consistent identification of the systemic discrepancy structure compared to other calibration approaches.

</details>


### [186] [The Impact of Temporal Granularity on Socio-Demographic Inference from Household Load Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03358)
*Dejan Radovanovic,Maximilian Schirl,Andreas Unterweger,Günther Eibl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能电表数据的时间分辨率（从15分钟到7天）如何影响对家庭社会人口学特征的推断性能，发现存在两个性能平台期（15分钟-1小时和1-7天），并揭示了不同属性对时间粒度的敏感度差异，为隐私-效用权衡提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明了智能电表数据可泄露家庭敏感信息，但时间分辨率如何影响推断性能尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用1,589个家庭一年数据，对8个社会人口学属性进行分类预测，比较从15分钟到7天的不同时间粒度；采用全年数据训练但在任意周测试的评估框架；对比手工特征、tsfresh特征与CNN自编码器嵌入，使用XGBoost等多种分类器。

Result: (1) 时间粗化降低预测精度，但存在两个平台期（15分钟-1小时和1-7天）；(2) 可解释手工特征和tsfresh特征与CNN自编码器嵌入性能相当，XGBoost优于其他分类器；(3) 静态属性（如 dwelling size）可从粗粒度数据推断，动态属性（如游泳池使用）需要细粒度时间信号。

Conclusion: 时间分辨率、特征提取方法和分类器选择共同影响社会人口学推断，存在数据最小化且不牺牲效用的机会，为智能电表隐私保护提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Smart meter data can reveal sensitive socio-demographic characteristics of households, raising privacy concerns. While this risk has been demonstrated at fixed granularities, the role of temporal resolution in shaping inference performance remains insufficiently explored. This paper addresses this gap by analyzing how load profiles with granularities from 15 minutes to 7 days affect the predictability of eight socio-demographic attributes in a dataset of 1,589 households over one year. We introduce an evaluation framework where classifiers are trained on year-round data but tested on arbitrary weeks, forcing generalization across seasonal and weekly variations. Our results show three main findings. First, while coarsening granularity reduces predictive accuracy, two plateaus emerge: performance is stable between 15 minutes and 1 hour, and again between 1 and 7 days. This reveals opportunities for data minimization without sacrificing utility. Second, interpretable handcrafted and tsfresh features remain competitive with CNN-based autoencoder embeddings, while XGBoost consistently outperforms alternative classifiers. Third, feature importance analysis highlights differences between static and dynamic attributes: dwelling size can be inferred even from coarse data, whereas swimming pool usage requires fine-grained temporal signals. Overall, our study provides new insights into the privacy-utility trade-off in smart metering, showing how temporal resolution, feature extraction, and classifier choice jointly influence socio-demographic inference.

</details>


### [187] [WUSH: Near-Optimal Adaptive Transforms for LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00956)
*Jiale Chen,Vage Egiazarian,Roberto L. Castro,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WUSH，一种结合Hadamard骨架与数据依赖二阶矩的非正交变换，实现了权重量化与激活量化的联合最优线性块变换，在W4A4量化下比最强Hadamard基线提升+2.8平均精度点（RTN），并实现最高5.8倍于BF16的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变换的量化缓解方法（如Hadamard旋转）是固定且数据无关的，其对量化的最优性尚不明确。本文旨在推导出联合权重-激活量化的闭式最优线性块变换。

Method: 推导了标准RTN AbsMax缩放块量化器下联合权重-激活量化的闭式最优线性块变换，提出WUSH：结合Hadamard骨架与数据依赖二阶矩分量，形成非正交变换，并设计了高效的融合GPU实现。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，W4A4量化下WUSH相比最强Hadamard基线：RTN提升+2.8平均精度点，GPTQ提升+0.7；FP4 MatMul吞吐量最高达BF16的5.8倍。

Conclusion: WUSH在FP和INT量化器下在温和假设下是近似最优的，且具有高效的实现，显著提升了低比特量化精度与效率。

Abstract: Quantizing LLM weights and activations is a standard approach for efficient deployment, but a few extreme outliers can stretch the dynamic range and amplify low-bit quantization errors. Prior transform-based mitigations (e.g., Hadamard rotations) are fixed and data-agnostic, and their optimality for quantization has remained unclear. We derive closed-form optimal linear blockwise transforms for joint weight-activation quantization under standard RTN AbsMax-scaled block quantizers, covering both integer and floating-point formats. The resulting construction, WUSH, combines a Hadamard backbone with a data-dependent second-moment component to form a non-orthogonal transform that is provably near-optimal for FP and INT quantizers under mild assumptions while admitting an efficient fused GPU implementation. Empirically, WUSH improves W4A4 accuracy over the strongest Hadamard-based baselines (e.g., on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct in MXFP4, it gains +2.8 average points with RTN and +0.7 with GPTQ) while delivering up to 5.8$\times$ per-layer throughput over BF16 via FP4 MatMul. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/WUSH.

</details>


### [188] [Mitigating False Credit Propagation: Probabilistic Graphical Reward Aggregation for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03361)
*Can Lv,Mingju Chen,Heng Chang,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEAR框架，通过概率图模型对评价准则间的依赖关系（前置条件、激活关系）进行建模，解决开放文本强化学习后训练中准则奖励的虚假传播问题，在多个基准上提升最高15.5%，减少96.5%的奖励泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于准则的奖励函数在聚合各维度分数时采用独立标量求和，忽略了准则间的前置条件和激活关系，导致奖励或惩罚在不该出现时被计入（称为虚假传播FCP）。

Method: 将每个准则结果建模为潜在伯努利事件，构建带类型的有向准则图，通过概率传播实现父事件未满足时抑制子事件的软惩罚，最终计算归一化期望带符号效用，实现线性时间复杂度的奖励计算，可嵌入标准基于准则的RL流程。

Result: 在HealthBench、WritingBench和PLawBench上，使用两个策略骨干网络，GEAR一致优于平面聚合和确定性门控方法，相对提升最高15.5%；FCP诊断显示GEAR相比平面聚合减少96.5%的奖励泄露，同时比确定性门控保留更多合法的下游效用。

Conclusion: GEAR是首个在准则奖励聚合中系统建模依赖关系的概率框架，有效解决了虚假传播问题，且计算开销小（线性时间），易于集成到现有强化学习训练流程中。

Abstract: Rubric-based rewards are increasingly used for open-ended language model post-training, but criterion-level scores are often aggregated as independent utilities. This flat scalarization ignores rubric-specified prerequisite and activation relations among criteria, allowing reward or penalty to be counted even when the condition that licenses it is absent. We call this structural reward-aggregation failure \textbf{False Credit Propagation} (FCP). To address this limitation, we propose \ourname (\textbf{G}raphical \textbf{E}vent \textbf{A}ggregation for \textbf{R}ubric rewards), a probabilistic graphical framework for dependency-aware rubric aggregation. \ourname models each criterion outcome as a latent Bernoulli event in a typed rubric graph, propagates soft suppression from unsupported parent events to their children, and aggregates the resulting event probabilities into a normalized expected signed utility. This yields a linear-time reward computation that can be plugged into standard rubric-based RL pipelines without changing the outer optimization algorithm. Experiments on HealthBench, WritingBench, and PLawBench with two policy backbones show that \ourname consistently improves over flat aggregation and deterministic gating, achieving relative gains of up to 15.5\% over flat aggregation. FCP diagnostics further show that \ourname reduces leakage by 96.5\% relative to flat aggregation while preserving more licensed downstream utility than deterministic gating. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LvCan926/GEAR.

</details>


### [189] [Link Prediction or Perdition: the Seeds of Instability in Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03365)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了知识图谱嵌入模型（KGEMs）在链接预测中的稳定性问题，发现高性能模型在三元组预测和嵌入空间组织上存在显著不稳定，随机种子等随机因素独立诱发同等量级的不稳定性，且高MRR的超参数配置并不保证更稳定，投票机制也只能有限提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估协议强调MRR、Hits@K等排序指标，但忽视了随机种子对结果稳定性的影响，且这些指标掩盖了单个预测和嵌入空间组织中的潜在不稳定性，因此需要对KGEMs进行系统的稳定性分析。

Method: 对多个KGEMs在多个数据集上进行系统性稳定性分析，通过隔离随机因素（初始化、三元组顺序、负采样、dropout、硬件）分别考察其对稳定性的影响，并评估投票机制作为补救措施的效果。

Result: 高性能模型在三元组级别产生分歧性预测且嵌入空间高度可变；每个随机因素独立诱发可比量级的不稳定性；高MRR配置不保证更稳定；投票机制只能提供有限的稳定性提升。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试协议存在关键局限性，对KGEMs用于知识图谱补全的可靠性提出质疑。

Abstract: Embedding models (KGEMs) constitute the main link prediction approach to complete knowledge graphs. Standard evaluation protocols emphasize rank-based metrics such as MRR or Hits@$K$, but usually overlook the influence of random seeds on result stability. Moreover, these metrics conceal potential instabilities in individual predictions and in the organization of embedding spaces. In this work, we conduct a systematic stability analysis of multiple KGEMs across several datasets. We find that high-performance models actually produce divergent predictions at the triple level and highly variable embedding spaces. By isolating stochastic factors (i.e., initialization, triple ordering, negative sampling, dropout, hardware), we show that each independently induces instability of comparable magnitude. Furthermore, for a given model, hyperparameter configurations with better MRR are not guaranteed to be more stable. Moreover, voting, albeit a known remediation mechanism, only provides a limited enhancement of stability. These findings highlight critical limitations of current benchmarking protocols, and raise concerns about the reliability of KGEMs for knowledge graph completion.

</details>


### [190] [Local Guidance, Global Impact: Gaussian-Reshaped Trust Region Unlocks Behavior Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03382)
*Bingxu Liu,Jiashun Liu,Johan Obando-Ceron,Hao Wang,Runze Liu,Pablo Samuel Castro,Aaron Courville,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯信任域策略优化（GTR），通过在非平稳环境中使用高斯核重塑信任域，解决了PPO在持续学习场景下更新效率低下和适应性不足的问题。GTR提供有界非单调约束，并结合混合高斯锚点降低参考陈旧性导致的方差，在游戏、机器人控制、开放世界探索和语言模型后训练等多个领域取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO在平稳环境中表现优异，但在持续性和非平稳环境中存在根本性失败：其优化范式执行持续、方向低效的局部更新，缺乏几何感知引导；基于散度的正则化虽引入部分几何感知，但其单调递增惩罚隐式抑制了必要的大策略偏移，阻碍对新行为模式的有效适应。

Method: 提出GTR方法：1）使用高斯核重塑信任域，生成有界非单调约束——提供强局部稳定性，同时在持续高优势更新的条件下渐进放松约束；2）引入混合高斯锚点，适应近期策略轨迹，降低陈旧参考导致的方差；3）该方法架构无关，可直接应用于多种强化学习场景。

Result: 在游戏、模拟机器人控制、开放世界探索和语言模型后训练等多个领域均取得强性能表现，证明了几何感知信任域设计在复杂非平稳环境中进行鲁棒强化学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 几何感知的信任域设计是解决非平稳环境中强化学习鲁棒性的有效方向，GTR通过有界非单调的约束机制在稳定性和适应性之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: While Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) demonstrates strong performance in stationary settings, we show that its standard optimization paradigm struggles in continual and non-stationary environments. The failure does not stem from insufficient model capacity or overly restrictive clipping. Instead, PPO performs persistent, directionally inefficient local updates, which indicates a lack of geometry-aware guidance for accumulating meaningful behavioral change and ultimately hindering transitions toward new behavior patterns. Although divergence-based regularization introduces partial geometric awareness, its monotonically increasing penalties implicitly discourage large policy deviations, even when such shifts are necessary for effective adaptation. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Trust Region Policy Optimization (GTR), which reshapes the trust region using a Gaussian kernel. The resulting constraint is bounded and non-monotonic, providing strong local stability while progressively relaxing under sustained high-advantage updates. To further improve robustness, we introduce a Mixture Gaussian Anchor that adapts to recent policy trajectories, reducing variance induced by stale references. GTR is architecture-agnostic and achieves strong performance across games, simulated robotic control, open-world exploration, and language model post-training. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware trust-region design can be a promising direction for robust reinforcement learning in complex non-stationary environments. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GTR_demo/README.md.

</details>


### [191] [When Model Merging Breaks Routing: Training-Free Calibration for MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03391)
*Canbin Huang,Tianyuan Shi,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Jianfei Zhang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE架构模型合并中的路由崩溃问题，提出了一种无需训练的Hessian感知路由器校准方法（HARC），利用二阶曲率信息通过闭式解和共轭梯度法高效对齐合并后的路由器，在数学推理和代码生成任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并技术（基于线性参数算术或优化）在应用于MoE架构时表现不佳，存在路由崩溃问题，即合并后的路由器无法将token正确分配给合适的专家，导致性能严重下降。

Method: 提出Hessian感知路由器校准（HARC），一种无需训练的方法，利用二阶曲率信息来重新对齐合并后的路由器，通过矩阵无关共轭梯度法高效求解闭式解。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上的实验表明，HARC有效缓解了多种MoE合并基线中的路由崩溃问题，带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: HARC是一种有效的、无需训练的MoE模型合并路由器校准方法，能够解决路由崩溃问题并提升合并模型性能。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a cost-effective approach for consolidating the capabilities of multiple LLMs without retraining. However, existing merging techniques, largely based on linear parameter arithmetic or optimization, struggle when applied to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures. We identify a critical failure mode in MoE merging, termed routing breakdown, in which the merged router fails to dispatch tokens to suitable experts. Routing breakdown stems from the sensitivity of the non-linear softmax and discrete Top-k routing mechanisms to parameter perturbations from merging, a sensitivity further amplified by load-balancing constraints imposed during MoE pretraining. Because fine-tuned experts exhibit distinct specializations, even modest misrouting can cause severe performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC), a training-free framework that leverages second-order curvature information to realign the merged router. This approach admits a closed-form solution that can be efficiently solved using a matrix-free conjugate gradient method. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks show that HARC effectively mitigates routing breakdown across diverse MoE merging baselines and leads to substantial performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/huangcb01/HARC.

</details>


### [192] [Flicker-DDPM: Accelerating Denoising Diffusion via 1/f Colored Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03393)
*Kexiang Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flicker-DDPM，一种使用1/fα彩色噪声替代标准白噪声的扩散模型，通过自组织临界性（SOC）启发的闪烁噪声匹配自然图像的频谱统计特性，在CIFAR-10上以3.33倍更少的采样步骤达到或超越标准DDPM的质量，并从理论上解释了频谱匹配的彩色噪声如何线性化反向轨迹从而实现采样加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDPM在前向过程中使用各向同性白噪声，这与自然图像功率谱服从P(k) ∝ 1/k^α的频谱统计特性不匹配，导致采样效率低下。作者受自然界广泛存在的自组织临界性（SOC）现象启发，提出使用具有幂律谱的彩色噪声来弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 基于空间相关核σ(d) = (d + 1)^{-η}构建彩色噪声模块，通过调节η控制生成1/f^α噪声的频谱指数α，适应不同数据集。2) 建立频域线性理论，证明频谱匹配的彩色噪声可线性化反向轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，Flicker-DDPM使用比标准DDPM少3.33倍的采样步骤即可匹配或超越其生成质量，且每步计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 频谱匹配的彩色噪声能够加速扩散模型的采样过程，其背后的理论机制在于线性化了反向轨迹；该工作为扩散模型中的噪声设计提供了新的理论视角和实践方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel diffusion model, Flicker-DDPM, which incorporates flicker (1/f) noise inspired by self-organized criticality (SOC), a widely observed phenomenon in natural systems. Unlike denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs), which employ isotropic white noise in the forward process, Flicker-DDPM adopts colored noise with power-law spectra to better match the spectral statistics of natural images, whose power spectra typically follow P(k) proportional to 1/k^α. To this end, we develop a colored-noise module based on a spatial correlation kernel, σ(d) = (d + 1)^{-η}, and theoretically establish that adjusting η controls the spectral exponent α of the generated 1/fα noise, enabling adaptation to datasets with diverse spectral characteristics. On CIFAR-10, Flicker DDPM matches or surpasses the generation quality of a standard DDPM baseline using 3.33 times fewer sampling steps, with negligible additional computational cost per step. We further develop a frequency-domain linear theory demonstrating that spectrally matched colored noise linearizes the reverse trajectory, theoretically explaining the observed sampling acceleration.

</details>


### [193] [KVarN: Variance-Normalized KV-Cache Quantization Mitigates Error Accumulation in Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03458)
*Lorenz K. Muller,Philippe Bich,Chiara Boretti,Hyun-Min Chang,Jiawei Zhuang,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KVarN，一种免校准的KV缓存量化方法，通过Hadamard旋转和双轴方差归一化解决自回归解码中量化误差累积问题，在2-bit精度下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展在长序列解码时受限于KV缓存的内存瓶颈，现有KV缓存量化方法主要在预填充场景下评估，而自回归解码中量化误差会随时间步累积，尤其是由错误的token尺度驱动，因此需要针对自回归场景的量化方法。

Method: 提出KVarN方法，包含两个关键步骤：1) 对K和V矩阵应用Hadamard旋转；2) 在K和V矩阵的两个轴上执行双尺度方差归一化（dual-scaling variance normalization），无需校准数据即可完成量化。

Result: 在MATH500、AIME24和HumanEval等生成式基准测试上，KVarN在2-bit精度下达到新的SOTA性能，显著优于现有基线方法，有效减少了误差累积。

Conclusion: KVarN通过Hadamard旋转结合双轴方差归一化，有效消除了异常token尺度误差，大幅降低了自回归解码中的量化误差累积，为KV缓存量化在生成任务中树立了新标杆。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is a powerful approach to obtain better reasoning in large language models, but it becomes memory-bottlenecked during long-horizon decoding, as the KV-cache grows. KV-cache quantization can help improve this, but current methods are evaluated under prefill-like settings and errors behave differently under autoregressive decoding. We show that in the latter regime, quantization errors accumulate across timesteps, driven primarily by incorrect token scales. We introduce KVarN, a calibration-free KV-cache quantizer that applies a Hadamard rotation followed by a dual-scaling variance normalization across both axes of the K and V matrices. We find that this combination fixes outlying token-scale errors and substantially reduces error accumulation over existing baselines. KVarN establishes a new state-of-theart for KV-cache quantization on generative benchmarks, including MATH500, AIME24 and HumanEval, at 2-bit precision. A vLLM implementation of the KVarN method is available at https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN

</details>


### [194] [Topology-Aware Gaussian Graph Repair for Robust Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03462)
*Anubha Goel,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAGR框架，通过自适应高斯核构建特征邻域图并结合拓扑感知残差校正来修复图结构，无需改变GNN架构即可提升模型在噪声边和缺失边场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在图拓扑不完美（含噪声边或缺失边）时性能下降，已有方法如移除边或学习新图结构存在局限性：移除边无法恢复缺失连接，图结构学习增加优化复杂度。

Method: TAGR包含两个组件：1) 自适应高斯核构建稀疏特征邻域图，为特征相似的节点引入辅助边；2) 拓扑感知残差校正，根据局部特征和结构一致性保留并重新加权原始图拓扑。修复后的图可直接用于标准GNN。

Result: 在基准引文网络上的实验表明，TAGR在噪声边和缺失边两种设置下均提升了GNN的鲁棒性。高斯特征邻域修复提供主要鲁棒性增益，拓扑感知残差校正在图不完整时提高稳定性。

Conclusion: 有效的图鲁棒性可以通过轻量级稀疏图修复实现，而非依赖密集图结构学习。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have achieved strong performance on graph-structured data, but their effectiveness depends heavily on the quality of the observed graph. In real applications, graph topology is often imperfect: noisy edges may connect unrelated nodes, while missing edges may prevent useful information from being propagated. Existing robust graph learning methods mainly address this problem by removing suspicious edges or by learning a new graph structure during training. However, edge removal alone cannot recover missing connections, and graph structure learning may introduce additional optimization complexity. In this paper, we propose Topology-Aware Gaussian Repair (TAGR), a simple graph repair framework for robust message passing in graph neural networks. Instead of learning a dense adjacency matrix, TAGR constructs a sparse feature-neighborhood graph using an adaptive Gaussian kernel and combines it with a topology-aware residual correction of the observed graph. The Gaussian repair component introduces auxiliary edges between feature-similar nodes, while the residual correction preserves and reweights the original topology according to local feature and structural consistency. The repaired graph can be used directly with standard graph neural networks without changing their architectures. Extensive experiments on benchmark citation networks show that TAGR improves the robustness of GNNs under both noisy-edge and missing-edge settings. The analysis further show that Gaussian feature-neighborhood repair provides the main robustness gain, while topology-aware residual correction improves stability when the observed graph is incomplete. These results suggest that effective graph robustness can be achieved through lightweight sparse graph repair rather than dense graph structure learning.

</details>


### [195] [Rethinking the Role of Tensor Decompositions in Post-Training LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03465)
*Artur Zagitov,Alexander Miasnikov,Maxim Krutikov,Vladimir Aletov,Gleb Molodtsov,Nail Bashirov,Artem Tsedenov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了张量分解在大型语言模型后训练压缩中的效果，发现张量分解假设的共享子空间与现代LLM学习的异构表示之间存在根本性不匹配，从而划定了其在实际大规模部署中的性能边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练压缩对于在严格资源约束下部署大型语言模型至关重要，张量分解被认为是一种有前景的方法，但现有研究仅在狭窄场景下评估，不清楚其在规模化部署中的有效性。

Method: 在密集和MoE架构上系统评估张量压缩方法，结合经验分析与理论分析建立性能权衡，并识别张量分解假设（共享子空间）与现代LLM学习特征（异构表示）之间的不匹配。

Result: 发现张量分解假设的共享子空间与现代LLM学习的异构表示之间存在根本性不匹配，从而划定了张量分解在大规模部署中的实际性能边界。

Conclusion: 张量分解在LLM压缩中的效果受限于其共享子空间假设与模型异构表示之间的根本性不匹配，这为其在大规模部署中的可行角色提供了清晰的边界。

Abstract: Post-training compression is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under tight resource constraints. Tensor decompositions have emerged as a promising direction, offering compact parameterizations well suited to Transformer weight structures. However, existing studies evaluate these methods in narrow settings, leaving unclear whether tensorization is effective at large-scale deployment. We systematically evaluate tensor compression across dense and MoE architectures, establishing performance trade-offs grounded in both empirical analysis and theoretical analysis. We identify a fundamental mismatch between the shared subspaces assumed by tensor decompositions and the heterogeneous representations learned by modern LLMs, thereby delineating their practical limits and clarifying their viable role in large-scale deployment. The code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/TT-LLM.

</details>


### [196] [Analyzing Stream Collapse in Hyper-Connections: From Diagnosis to Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03483)
*Ekaterina Alimaskina,Gleb Molodtsov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Hyper-Connections(HC)中多流表示的对称性破解问题，发现残差混合接近恒等映射、信号集中在主导流中导致多流容量未被充分利用。通过打破初始化对称性可减少主导行为并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究HC中多流置换对称性在实际训练中如何被破解：各流是均衡特化还是出现主导流占用，以及多流残差连接是否有效利用了其容量。

Method: 对基于HC的语言模型进行细粒度诊断分析，追踪多流表示的实际使用方式，并通过在初始化阶段打破对称性来改进mHC变体。

Result: 发现：1)早期播种阶段后残差混合接近恒等映射；2)信号和可解释特征集中在主导流中；3)多流残差连接行为接近单流；4)打破初始化对称性可减少主导行为并提升性能。

Conclusion: HC的多流容量在实际中未被充分利用，通过初始化阶段打破对称性可以缓解主导流问题并提升模型性能。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) replace the single Transformer residual stream with multiple streams, introducing a permutation symmetry over stream indices. We study how this symmetry is resolved in practice: whether streams specialize in a balanced way or exhibit dominant-stream usage. Using fine-grained diagnostics for HC-based language models, we trace how multi-stream representations are actually used. We find that after an early seeding stage, residual mixing often remains close to identity, limiting a core HC mechanism for exchanging information between streams. Moreover, both signal and interpretable features concentrate in a dominant stream, and the nominally multi-stream residual connection can underutilize its capacity, behaving closer to a single-stream residual pathway. Finally, we show that breaking symmetry at stream initialization reduces dominant behavior and improves performance across \textit{m}HC variants. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [197] [Demystifying Pipeline Parallelism: First Theory for PipeDream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03498)
*Ivan Ilin,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对PipeDream流水线模型并行方法进行了理论分析，提出了Randomized PipeDream (RPD)算法并给出了首个非凸收敛保证；推导了稳态延迟与阶段数S的平方关系，导致梯度陈旧项以Θ(γ²S⁴)增长；通过实验比较了PD与LocalSGD在不同目标函数下的性能表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习训练需要跨多个加速器分布式计算，当参数、激活值或优化器状态无法容纳在单设备时，模型并行不可避免。本文旨在从理论层面深入理解PipeDream这一流水线模型并行方法，为其收敛性提供严格保证，并诊断其扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出了Randomized PipeDream (RPD)，一种陈旧块SGD抽象，用于分析PD风格方法的收敛性；2) 推导了稳态PD中延迟与阶段数S的解析表达式(S² - S/2 + O(1))；3) 建立了梯度陈旧项与阶段数的缩放关系(Θ(γ²S⁴)或Θ(S⁴/K))；4) 在模拟时间实验中对比PD与LocalSGD在二次目标、小语言模型训练损失和逻辑回归上的表现。

Result: RPD给出了首个PD风格方法的非凸收敛保证；稳态延迟随阶段数S呈二次增长(S²)；梯度陈旧贡献以Θ(γ²S⁴)增长；实验表明PD在二次目标和小语言模型任务上更优，而LocalSGD在逻辑回归任务上随阶段数增加表现更好。

Conclusion: PD与LocalSGD的优劣取决于具体目标函数：PD在二次目标和小语言模型上表现更好，LocalSGD在逻辑回归上随阶段数增加更优。这为分布式训练中方法选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Training modern machine learning models increasingly requires computation to be distributed across many accelerators. Data parallelism remains the default choice and is often paired with tensor-parallel sharding, but model parallelism becomes unavoidable once parameters, activations, or optimizer states no longer fit on a single device. This paper studies pipeline model parallelism through the lens of PipeDream (PD) (Harlap et al., 2018). Our first contribution is theoretical: we introduce Randomized PipeDream (RPD), a stale block-SGD abstraction that yields, to our knowledge, the first clean nonconvex convergence guarantee for a PD-style method. Our second contribution is a scaling diagnosis: we prove that the delay induced by steady-state PD grows as $S^2 - S/2 + O(1)$ for $S$ stages, so the stale-read contribution in the convergence theorem scales as $Θ(γ^2 S^4)$, equivalently as $Θ(S^4/K)$ in the tuned-rate form. Our third contribution is a comparison with LocalSGD, whose periodic model averaging trades weight staleness for synchronization bubbles. In our reported simulated-time experiments, the better-performing method depends on the objective: PD performs better on the quadratic objective and on a small language-modeling training-loss task, while for logistic regression LocalSGD becomes superior as the number of stages increases.

</details>


### [198] [Post-Hoc Robustness for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03521)
*Siemen Herremans,Ali Anwar,Siegfried Mercelis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在推理阶段提升深度强化学习智能体鲁棒性的后处理方法，该方法利用学习到的模型与名义策略，通过对抗性模型预测控制（MPC）进行策略改进，无需额外训练神经网络，在MuJoCo环境中显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升强化学习在真实世界中的适用性，尤其关注在环境扰动下训练鲁棒智能体的问题。现有对抗性鲁棒RL方法需要额外训练，本文希望在不进行额外神经网络训练的前提下，在推理阶段提升策略的鲁棒性。

Method: 利用已学习的环境模型与训练好的名义策略，在推理阶段执行鲁棒策略改进步骤。具体采用模型预测控制（MPC）进行对抗性轨迹展开，通过投影梯度下降在有界不确定性集内近似对抗扰动，并处理离线轨迹展开中的分布外（OOD）问题。

Result: 在受扰动的Gymnasium MuJoCo环境中，该方法在考虑后推理阶段计算限制的情况下，显著提升了算法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 后验鲁棒化方法能够在不额外训练神经网络的前提下有效提升深度RL智能体的鲁棒性，为对抗性鲁棒RL提供了一种实用的推理时增强策略。

Abstract: To improve the real-world applicability of reinforcement learning (RL), the field of adversarially robust RL studies how to train agents under adversarial environment perturbations. In this setting, a protagonist agent optimizes a policy under environmental perturbations from an adversary, resulting in a zero-sum Markov game. When adversarially robust RL is combined with model-based RL, the adversary can target a learned transition model instead of the training environment. Extending this idea, this work introduces post-hoc robustification of deep RL agents at inference time. By using the learned model in combination with a trained nominal policy, our approach performs a robust policy improvement step. The goal is to improve robustness without any additional training of neural networks. Specifically, we utilize model-predictive control under adversarial rollouts, which are approximated via projected gradient descent within a bounded uncertainty set. Furthermore, these offline rollouts are performed while considering and mitigating out-of-distribution issues. The proposed methodology is validated by demonstrating significant improvements in robustness when the algorithm is evaluated in perturbed Gymnasium MuJoCo environments, while considering the computational limitations of the post-hoc inference setting.

</details>


### [199] [When Should the Teacher Move? Temporal Coupling and Stability in Self On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03532)
*Haowei Guo,Baolong Bi,Ruicheng Zhang,Bingqian Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自在线策略蒸馏中教师更新调度的影响，发现冻结期（isolation periods）是稳定学习的关键，而非教师年龄。提出诊断框架揭示“状态遗忘性崩溃”（state-oblivious collapse）——短视固定调度在长程训练中因时钟驱动的单步刷新而灾难性失败。为解决问题，提出Consolidation-Gated Teacher Refresh（CGTR），通过基于奖励提升和尾部安全的联合门控来保留冻结期，在四个任务上实现零崩溃和最优性能，且无需每数据集重调参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自在线策略蒸馏中教师更新调度（temporal coupling）作为稳定变量的作用尚未被系统研究，现有工作对教师冻结期等结构属性的重要性缺乏认识。

Method: 通过Qwen3-8B上的控制实验进行调度扫描，提出时序KL结构、刷新冲击、长度尾部风险等诊断框架；设计Consolidation-Gated Teacher Refresh（CGTR），在保留冻结期的同时基于奖励提升和长度尾部安全联合门控教师刷新。

Result: CGTR在Chemistry、Biology、Physics、ToolUse四个任务上实现零崩溃和最佳最终得分，且能根据各任务学习动态自调节刷新频率，无需每数据集重新调参。

Conclusion: 教师更新的冻结期是自在线策略蒸馏中稳定学习的关键结构属性；时钟驱动的固定刷新会导致状态遗忘性崩溃，而CGTR通过基于学生巩固证据的门控机制有效避免了这一问题。

Abstract: Self on-policy distillation trains a student policy against a teacher derived from its own parameter history, yet the teacher's update schedule -- which governs the \emph{temporal coupling} between teacher and student -- has not been systematically studied as a stability variable. Through a controlled schedule sweep on Qwen3-8B, we establish that \emph{isolation periods}, defined as complete teacher freezing between updates, are the key structural property enabling stable learning, not teacher age. To characterize these underlying training dynamics, we introduce a diagnostic framework of temporal KL structure, refresh shock, and length-tail risk. This framework further uncovers \emph{state-oblivious collapse}: optimal short-horizon fixed schedules catastrophically fail under long-horizon training because a clock-driven refresh can copy a transiently drifting student into the teacher in a single, irreversible step. This failure mode is invisible under short-horizon evaluation and mechanistically distinct from EMA's chronic contamination. To address this, we propose \emph{Consolidation-Gated Teacher Refresh} (CGTR), which preserves isolation periods while gating each refresh on joint evidence of reward improvement and length-tail safety, ensuring every teacher movement responds to genuine student consolidation rather than a clock signal. With a single shared parameter set and no per-dataset retuning, CGTR achieves \textbf{zero collapse} and the best final score on all four tasks (Chemistry, Biology, Physics, ToolUse), self-regulating its refresh frequency to each task's learning dynamics.

</details>


### [200] [How Many Trees in a Random Forest? A Revisited Approach with Plateau Search and Optuna Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03549)
*Vadim Porvatov,Andrey Dukhovny,Andrey Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对随机森林超参数优化中树的数量难以调优的问题（预测分数随集成规模单调递增，标准方法易偏向边界），提出一种基于三元组的平台搜索算法，将树的数量从TPE搜索空间中移除，通过监控OOB分数的相对变化自适应追踪近最小充分集成大小，并提供理论分析和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林HPO中树的数量调优存在特殊困难：预测分数随集成规模单调递增，导致TPE、Hyperband等标准方法需要预定义搜索范围且常将估计推向右侧边界；早期停止策略虽避免固定范围，但对分数噪声敏感且易过早停止。

Method: 提出集成三元组平台搜索算法：1) 将树的数量从TPE直接搜索空间中移除；2) 通过监控三个森林规模（三元组）的OOB分数相对变化，自适应地移动三元组来追踪近最小充分集成大小；3) 基于容差参数实现自动化且可解释的流程；4) 提供理论分析，将相对OOB分数准则与当前分数和极限分数之间的差距联系起来，并推导OOB绝对相对差异的渐近方差估计。

Result: 实验表明选择的树数量与常见启发式方法有显著差异：对大多数经典基准数据集更小，而对Arcene、Dorothea等高维生物信息学数据集则更大。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种自动化、可解释的随机森林树数量调优方案，避免了预定义搜索范围和早期停止的缺陷，并在不同数据集上展现出适应性。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) for Random Forest faces a specific difficulty in tuning the number of trees: the predictive score typically improves monotonically with ensemble size, so standard methods such as Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) and Hyperband require a predefined search range and often drive the estimate toward its right boundary. Early-stopping strategies avoid fixing such a range, but can be sensitive to score noise and prone to premature stopping. To address this, we propose an integrated triplet-based plateau-search algorithm that removes the number of trees from the direct TPE search space and still exploits information accumulated across HPO trials. The method adaptively tracks a near-minimal sufficient ensemble size by monitoring relative changes in the out-of-bag (OOB) score across a triplet of forest sizes and shifting this triplet accordingly. This yields an automated and user-interpretable procedure based on a tolerance parameter. We also provide a theoretical analysis: we relate the proposed relative OOB-score criterion to the gap between the current and limiting scores, and derive an asymptotic variance estimate for the corresponding OOB-based absolute relative difference. Experiments show that the selected number of trees can differ substantially from the common heuristic: for most classical benchmark datasets it is smaller, whereas for some high-dimensional bioinformatics datasets, such as Arcene and Dorothea, it is larger. The source code and reproducible experiments are available at https://github.com/lange-am/rf_plateau_hpo.

</details>


### [201] [CauTion: Knowing When to Trust LLMs for Ensemble Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03602)
*Bo Peng,Kaiwen Wu,Sirui Chen,Zhiheng Wang,Yu Qiao,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CauTion是一个将LLM领域知识与统计因果发现算法集成框架，通过共识过滤、信任校准仲裁和环修复三个阶段，在六个数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯统计因果发现方法存在统计等价类内不可区分性和小样本敏感性的根本局限，而现有LLM增强方法易受LLM错误影响、token成本高，且依赖单一数据算法会产生算法特定偏差。

Method: CauTion包含三个阶段：1) 算法集成通过共识投票解决算法一致的边（达96%），达到近乎完美准确率；2) 通过免标注信任校准估计LLM和算法的相对可靠性，限制LLM仲裁仅对算法证据不可靠的边进行信任加权投票；3) 环修复步骤确保最终因果图无环。

Result: 在六个数据集上，CauTion一致优于纯数据驱动和LLM增强基线方法，在大图上增益更大，对LLM错误具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CauTion通过可靠的集成策略有效结合LLM领域知识与统计因果发现，克服了各自的局限性，实现了更准确和鲁棒的因果发现。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the fundamental limitations of purely statistical methods, such as statistical distinguishability within equivalence classes and sensitivity to finite sample sizes. While large language models (LLMs) offer a promising source of domain knowledge to complement statistical inference, existing LLM-augmented methods are vulnerable to LLM errors and incur high token costs. Moreover, reliance on a single data-centric algorithm can make results sensitive to algorithm-specific biases. To address these limitations, we propose CauTion, a framework that reliably integrates LLM domain knowledge into an ensemble of statistical causal discovery algorithms through consensus filtering and LLM reliability estimation. CauTion proceeds in three stages. First, an algorithm ensemble utilizes a consensus voting to resolve up to 96% of edges on which algorithms agree, achieving near-perfect accuracy on the filtered consensus edges. Second, a trust-calibrated arbitration mechanism estimates the relative reliability of the LLM and the algorithms via an annotation-free trust calibration procedure, which is then utilized to govern a trust-weighted voting process that restricts LLM arbitration exclusively to edges with unreliable algorithmic evidence. Third, a cycle repair step is applied to guarantee the final causal graph is validly acyclic. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate that CauTion consistently outperforms both data-centric and LLM-augmented baselines, with larger gains on larger graphs and strong robustness to LLM errors. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauTion.

</details>


### [202] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高斯径向基核（RBF）支持向量回归（SVR）的非凸预测函数优化问题，提出了一种利用DC分解的DC算法框架，推导了强凸性下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，两者均共享主导项Cαρ。实验表明Cαρ是表征DCA收敛性和初始点依赖性的单一关键量，且可分解为C→Cα和γ→ρ两条独立路径，从而允许在训练前后通过标量Cαρ预先评估DCA的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RBF-SVR模型预测函数对应的非凸优化问题，现有DCA方法缺乏对收敛性能的解析刻画，难以在训练前评估算法行为，因此需要建立收敛性质与模型超参数之间的显式解析关系。

Method: 利用RBF核的解析结构构造显式DC分解，推导强凸性参数下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，二者均由训练后对偶系数和Cα、RBF核参数γ及DC分解参数ρ决定，共享主导项Cαρ。通过六个基准函数上的数值实验验证Cαρ对收敛性和初始点依赖性的表征作用。

Result: 发现Cαρ是表征DCA收敛性质和初始点依赖性的单一主要量；Cαρ可分解为C→Cα和γ→ρ两条独立路径，其变化主要由SVR超参数(C,γ)主导；训练前可近似评估，训练后可精确闭式计算。

Conclusion: 通过单一标量Cαρ即可提前评估DCA在RBF-SVR上的收敛性能，建立了从超参数到算法收敛性的直接解析桥梁。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [203] [Training a Predictive Coding Network on ImageNet using Equilibrium Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03584)
*Tugdual Kerjan,Rasmus Høier,Benjamin Scellier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合居中均衡传播（Centered EP）和新颖平衡方案的预测编码网络（PCN）训练方法，首次在ImageNet规模上成功训练了10层卷积PCN（VGG10），top-5错误率13.23%，接近反向传播基线12.2%。这是PCN和EP方法在大规模任务上的首次验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均衡传播（EP）和预测编码网络（PCN）此前仅在小规模问题上得到验证，缺乏在大规模实际任务中的可行性证明。本文旨在突破这两种方法的规模化限制，探索其在大规模图像分类任务上的潜力。

Method: 将居中均衡传播（Centered EP）与针对PCN设计的新型平衡方案相结合，开发出一种基于EP的PCN训练方法，并在10层卷积网络（VGG10）上使用完整ImageNet数据集进行训练。

Result: 在ImageNet top-5分类任务上，10层卷积PCN达到13.23%的测试错误率，与反向传播基线（12.2%）接近。

Conclusion: 这是PCN和基于EP的训练方法首次在ImageNet规模上成功演示，显著扩展了两种方法的可扩展性。结论暗示，EP在其他物理系统中规模化的主要挑战可能更多来自这些系统的计算特性，而非EP框架本身的固有局限。

Abstract: Equilibrium Propagation (EP) is a physics-based training framework that has primarily been employed in energy-based models, including continuous Hopfield networks, nonlinear resistive networks and coupled phase oscillators. However, EP's practical applications have so far remained limited to relatively small-scale problems. Predictive coding networks (PCNs), another class of energy-based models rooted in computational neuroscience, are typically trained with a specialized algorithm and have likewise not yet been demonstrated at large scale. In this work, we develop an EP-based training method for PCNs which combines the centered variant of EP with a novel equilibration scheme for PCNs. Using this approach, we train a 10-layer convolutional PCN (VGG10) on full-size ImageNet, achieving 13.23\% test error rate on the top-5 classification task, close to the 12.2\% backpropagation baseline. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of both PCNs and EP-based training at ImageNet scale. These results significantly extend the scalability of both approaches and suggest that the primary challenges in scaling EP in other physical systems may come more from the computational properties of these systems than from inherent limitations of the EP framework.

</details>


### [204] [Dynamic Short Convolutions Improve Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03825)
*Oliver Sieberling,Bharat Runwal,Rameswar Panda,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态短卷积（dynamic short convolutions）作为一种新的神经网络原语，用于改进Transformer架构。与静态短卷积不同，动态卷积使用依赖于输入的滤波器，在保留卷积局部性偏差的同时提高表达能力。实验表明，在150M到2B参数的语言模型中，动态卷积一致优于标准Transformer和静态短卷积增强的Transformer，在计算匹配下获得1.33×到1.60×的计算优势，同时在线性RNN和MoE架构上也有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer已成为大语言模型的主导架构，但现有的注意力机制和前馈层仍有改进空间。静态卷积缺乏输入依赖性，表达能力有限。作者希望引入一种既能保留卷积局部性偏差又能根据输入动态调整的卷积原语，以提升Transformer的性能和计算效率。

Method: 提出动态短卷积作为新的网络原语，其滤波器依赖于输入（input-dependent）。将动态短卷积应用于key、query和value表示，以及每个线性层之后。使用自定义Triton内核实现高效的端到端训练。

Result: 在联想回忆任务上，动态卷积优于静态卷积变体。在150M到2B参数的语言模型实验中，动态卷积一致超越标准Transformer和静态短卷积增强的Transformer。拟合标度律显示：对KQV应用动态卷积获得1.33×计算优势，每个线性层后添加获得1.60×优势。在Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet和MoE架构上也有改进。

Conclusion: 动态短卷积是一种可扩展、硬件高效且富有表达能力的原语，能够有效推进基于Transformer的语言模型的发展。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture for large language models, largely due to the scalability and flexibility of attention, feed-forward layers, residual connections, and normalization. This paper introduces dynamic short convolutions as an additional neural network primitive for improving Transformers. Unlike static short convolutions, dynamic convolutions use input-dependent filters, which preserves the locality bias of convolution while increasing expressivity. Motivating experiments show that applying dynamic short convolutions to key, query, and value representations improves performance on challenging associative recall tasks compared with static convolutional variants. Across language-modeling experiments ranging from 150M to 2B parameters, dynamic convolutions consistently outperform standard Transformers and Transformers augmented with static short convolutions. Fitting scaling laws indicates a 1.33$\times$ compute advantage over compute-matched Transformers when dynamic convolutions are applied to the key, query, and value vectors, and a 1.60$\times$ advantage when adding dynamic convolutions after every linear layer. Dynamic convolutions also offer improvements on linear RNNs (Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet) and mixture-of-experts architectures. We make these gains practical with custom Triton kernels that enable efficient training with a manageable end-to-end slowdown. These results suggest that dynamic short convolutions are a scalable, hardware-efficient, and expressive primitive for advancing Transformer-based language models.

</details>


### [205] [Exploiting Verification-Generation Gap: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03608)
*Jiahui Li,Jianfeng Shan,Wenpei Chen,Shunyu Wu,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng,Dan Li,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，通过置信度条件化验证机制，在无标签环境下同时提升大语言模型推理的Pass@1和Pass@k性能，在6个基准上平均超越现有方法+9.8% Pass@1和+18.7% Pass@16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习研究主要关注Pass@1性能，而Pass@k（衡量生成覆盖率的指标）在无标签环境中的优化被忽视，且直接应用RLVR的Pass@k优势设计效果不佳。通过实证分析发现，低置信度样本的伪标签估计错误率高，高置信度样本的候选答案多样性崩溃。

Method: 提出TTRL-CoCoV置信度自适应框架：1）高置信度样本：引导验证器并应用探索增强奖励防止多样性崩溃；2）低置信度样本：将伪标签选择委托给验证器以过滤错误标签；3）中等置信度样本：跳过验证。核心洞察是验证能力通常引导生成能力。

Result: 在6个广泛认可的基准上，TTRL-CoCoV在Pass@1上平均绝对提升+9.8%，Pass@16上平均绝对提升+18.7%（对比TTRL），甚至在与全监督RL方法对比时在多个推理基准上实现+5.0%的Pass@1绝对提升。

Conclusion: 置信度条件化的验证机制能有效解决无标签环境下Pass@k优化的两大难题（伪标签错误与多样性崩溃），验证能力与生成能力的协同关系值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning abilities of large language models in a completely label-free manner. Despite existing studies focusing on Pass@1 performance, optimizing Pass@k remains under-explored yet critical in label-free settings, which measures generation coverage for sustained exploration. Optimizing Pass@k in label-free setting is highly non-trivial, as directly applying the Pass@k advantage designs effective for RLVR yields unsatisfactory performance. Through in-depth empirical analysis, we discover the root causes hindering performance: pseudo-label estimations for low-confidence samples have a high probability of being incorrect, while candidate answers for high-confidence samples suffer from severe diversity collapse. To overcome these hurdles, we propose TTRL-CoCoV (Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification), a novel confidence-adaptive framework that expands Pass@k coverage and improves Pass@1 performance. Based on our key insight that verification capability generally leads generation capability, TTRL-CoCoV employs a confidence-conditioned mechanism: for high-confidence samples, it bootstraps verifier and applies an exploration-enhancing reward to prevent diversity collapse; for low-confidence samples, it delegates pseudo-label selection to the verifier to filter incorrect pseudo-labels; and for medium-confidence samples, it bypasses verification entirely. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TTRL-CoCoV outperforms the best competing methods across 6 widely-recognized benchmarks, achieves average absolute gains of +9.8% in Pass@1 and +18.7% in Pass@16 over TTRL, and even achieves absolute Pass@1 improvements of up to +5.0% across multiple reasoning benchmarks when compared against fully supervised RL methods. Our code repository: https://github.com/shanjf666/CoCoV.

</details>


### [206] [Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03089)
*Ming Wen,Yuxuan Liu,Kun Yang,Yunhao Feng,Zhuoer Xu,Yuhao Sun,Shiwen Cui,Xiang Zheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: on-policy self-distillation在安全对齐中仍会崩溃，原因在于宪法条件化导致教师分布收缩到短且过度保守的回复，Reverse KL进一步放大这种收缩。本文提出COPSD方法，通过Cross-SFT冷启动校准教师，再进行宪法条件化的on-policy蒸馏，在12个基准上实现了更好的安全-有用性权衡，并减少了安全税对通用推理能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD)在可验证推理任务中已被发现会崩溃，但安全对齐场景由于是高层级宪法引导而非显式目标答案，看起来更适合密集蒸馏。然而初步研究表明安全OPSD仍然存在严重崩溃问题，需要分析和解决这一现象。

Method: 首先通过几何泄漏分析形式化了安全OPSD崩溃的机理：在非正交语义空间中，安全压力会转移到表达能力维度。基于此提出COPSD方法：(1) Cross-SFT冷启动校准教师模型；(2) 宪法条件化的on-policy蒸馏。

Result: 在12个基准上，COPSD相比基线方法实现了更强且一致的安全-有用性权衡，同时显著减少了安全税对通用推理能力的影响。

Conclusion: 安全对齐场景下的on-policy蒸馏需要专门的校准和约束机制来避免分布崩溃，COPSD通过Cross-SFT冷启动和宪法条件化蒸馏有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as an efficient post-training paradigm by using a teacher conditioned on privileged information to provide dense token-level supervision. Prior work has shown that OPSD can collapse in verifiable reasoning tasks, but safety alignment differs in that it is guided by high-level constitutions rather than explicit target answers, making it a natural setting to revisit dense distillation. However, our pilot study show that safety OPSD still suffers from severe collapse: constitutional conditioning contracts the teacher distribution toward short and overly conservative responses, and Reverse KL further amplifies this contraction into reduced expressiveness. We formalize this effect as geometric leakage under safety boundaries in a non-orthogonal semantic space, where safety pressure transfers into the expressiveness dimension. Based on this analysis, we propose Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation (COPSD), which first calibrates the teacher through a Cross-SFT cold-start and then performs constitution-conditioned on-policy distillation. Experiments on 12 benchmarks show that COPSD achieves a consistently stronger safety--helpfulness trade-off than baselines while substantially reducing the safety tax on general reasoning ability.

</details>


### [207] [Physics-Guided Policy Optimization with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03620)
*Ke Wang,Yuning Wu,Haoran Liu,Chaoqun Jia,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的策略优化（PGPO），通过互信息估计动态调节自蒸馏策略优化的步长，在Science-QA上相比SDPO提升达4.5个点且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO对每步更新的信任度敏感，自教师的纠正有些批次信息量大、有些具有误导性，固定步长统一应用会导致训练不稳定。

Method: 受粘性流体动力学启发，在SDE层面形式化类比，提出PGPO。引入基于学生预测与反馈条件教师之间互信息估计的信息调制步长乘子，并证明该调制保持vanilla SGD的一阶弱近似保证，每轮迭代开销可忽略。

Result: 在Science-QA数据集上，PGPO在4个域中的3个优于SDPO，提升高达+4.5点；在SDPO训练后期崩溃的场景中PGPO保持稳定。

Conclusion: PGPO通过互信息调制的动态步长机制有效解决了SDPO对固定步长的敏感性问题，在提升性能的同时增强了训练稳定性。

Abstract: Self-distilled policy optimization (SDPO) has become a popular paradigm for LLM post-training, where a model learns from its own predictions conditioned on privileged information. SDPO, however, is sensitive to how much each update step should be trusted: corrections from a self-teacher can be highly informative on some batches and misleading on others, and applying them uniformly with a fixed step size can destabilize training. Drawing inspiration from viscous-fluid dynamics and formalizing the analogy at the SDE level, we propose Physics-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO), which introduces an information-modulated step-size multiplier derived from a mutual-information estimate between the student's predictions and the feedback-conditioned teacher. We show that this modulation preserves the order-1 weak-approximation guarantees of vanilla SGD, and incurs negligible overhead per iteration. We evaluate PGPO on the Science-QA dataset, where it outperforms SDPO on 3 of the 4 domains with gains of up to +4.5 points, while remaining stable in a setting where SDPO collapses late in training.

</details>


### [208] [Skill-RM: Unifying Heterogeneous Evaluation Criteria via Agent Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03980)
*Tao Chen,Gangwei Jiang,Pengyu Cheng,Siyuan Huang,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Jiaqi Guo,Mengyu Zhou,Kai Tang,Junling Liu,Qinliang Su,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-RM，将奖励建模重构为可复用的奖励评估技能执行过程，通过统一接口动态编排异构证据资源，在奖励评估和下游任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前奖励评估依赖规则验证器、真值参考、程序清单和复杂评分标准等异构标准，缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据。

Method: 将奖励计算视为结构化的智能体任务，通过统一的奖励评估技能接口动态选择和聚合针对每个输入的特定证据。

Result: 在奖励基准测试和下游应用（包括Best-of-N选择与强化学习）中，Skill-RM consistently优于传统judge基线。

Conclusion: Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，而且通过策略性动态编排证据实现了更优性能。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) provide critical feedback signals for LLM post-training, notably in reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines. However, current reward evaluation relies on heterogeneous criteria such as rule-based verifiers, ground-truth references, procedural checklists, and complex rubrics, where a unified mechanism to integrate all types of evidence remains unexplored. To this end, we propose Skill Reward Model (Skill-RM), a unified framework that reformulates reward modeling as the execution of a reusable Reward-Evaluation Skill. By treating reward computation as a structured agentic task, Skill-RM provides a consistent interface to orchestrate heterogeneous resources, dynamically selecting and aggregating evidence tailored to the specific requirements of each input. This approach enables the reward model to move beyond static evaluation, ensuring consistency and transparency across diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on reward benchmarks and downstream applications, including best-of-N selection and reinforcement learning, demonstrate that Skill-RM consistently outperforms traditional judge baselines. Our findings suggest that Skill-RM not only provides a unified solution for reward modeling but also achieves superior performance through the strategic and dynamic orchestration of evidence. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM.

</details>


### [209] [AnchorMoE: Interpretable Time Series Classification via Anchor-Routed MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03631)
*Tao Xie,Zexi Tan,Haoyi Xiao,Mengke Li,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Lu,Cuie Yang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorMoE，一个基于混合专家架构的可解释多变量时间序列分类框架，通过局部补丁的多视图表示路由到专门专家，实现输入段上的精确加性分解，并引入几何正交约束和不确定性感知门控机制，在保持高分类性能的同时提供事前透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列分类在高风险领域至关重要，但判别性信号稀疏、异构且被背景噪声掩盖，现有方法难以隔离驱动模型预测的时间片段，且依赖事后解释（post-hoc）方法缺乏可靠性。

Method: 1) 基于MoE架构，编码局部补丁的多视图表示并路由到专门专家；2) 引入几何正交约束惩罚表示冗余，迫使不同专家专业化于异构预测模式；3) 设计不确定性感知可靠性门控，动态校准各片段的贡献以抑制背景噪声；4) 最终预测形式化为输入片段上的精确加性分解，实现事前（ante-hoc）透明性。

Result: 在真实世界和合成基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AnchorMoE在达到高度竞争的分类性能的同时，能够忠实地将决策归因于原始时间序列。

Conclusion: AnchorMoE通过将预测分解为输入段的可解释加性组合，有效解决了MTSC中的可解释性挑战，实现了分类性能与透明性的兼顾。

Abstract: Multivariate time series classification (MTSC) is pivotal in high-stakes domains, such as clinical diagnosis and industrial fault detection, where safe deployment necessitates transparent decision-making. However, isolating the temporal segments that drive model predictions is challenging because discriminative signals in real-world time series are typically sparse, heterogeneous, and heavily obscured by background noise. This paper, therefore, proposes AnchorMoE, an interpretable-by-construction classification framework. Built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, AnchorMoE encodes multi-view representations of local patches and routes them to specialized experts, ensuring that the final prediction is formulated as an exact additive decomposition over the input segments, facilitating ante-hoc transparency rather than relying on post-hoc estimations. To maintain the reliability of this decomposition under sparse signal distributions, we introduce a geometric orthogonality constraint that penalizes representational redundancy, compelling distinct experts to specialize in heterogeneous predictive patterns. Furthermore, an uncertainty-aware reliability gate is designed to dynamically calibrate the contribution of each segment, effectively suppressing residual background noise. Extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that AnchorMoE achieves highly competitive classification performance while faithfully grounding its decisions in the raw time series.

</details>


### [210] [Spatial Transcriptomics-Guided Alignment Enhances Molecular Profiling in Pathology Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03644)
*Fengtao Zhou,Yingxue Xu,Zhengyu Zhang,Yihui Wang,Zhengrui Guo,Ling Liang,Jiabo Ma,Cheng Jin,Ziyi Liu,Huajun Zhou,Hongyi Wang,Du Cai,Chenglong Zhao,Xi Wang,Can Yang,Yu Wang,Wenbin Li,Feng Gao,Zhe Wang,Zhenhui Li,Xiuming Zhang,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP是一种利用空间转录组学引导的对齐框架，通过路径信息整合策略将H&E组织病理图像与转录组图谱对齐，赋予病理基础模型内在的分子感知能力，从而突破当前PFMs缺乏空间分辨分子监督的局限，并在临床分子分型任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学亟需全面的分子分析，但受限于成本高、标本耗尽和周转时间长。现有的病理基础模型（PFMs）主要依赖视觉自监督学习或视觉-语言对齐，缺乏将细微形态特征与潜在基因组改变联系起来所需的空间分辨分子监督。

Method: 构建了HumanST-1k数据集（涵盖多种人体器官和测序平台，含180万对H&E斑块-转录组图谱）；采用路径信息对齐策略（Pathway-informed Alignment），将原始转录组数据聚合为生物功能通路以降低技术噪声；通过参数高效微调将通路信息整合到PFMs中。

Result: STAMP对齐后的PFMs表征空间得到丰富，解锁了其解析亚视觉分子特征的能力，并通过多层次评估框架验证了其临床实用性。

Conclusion: 空间转录组学引导的对齐策略能够有效赋予病理基础模型内在的分子感知能力，为低成本、高效能的精准分子分型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Comprehensive molecular profiling is essential for modern precision oncology but remains hindered by prohibitive costs, specimen exhaustion, and protracted turnaround times. While pathology foundation models (PFMs) have demonstrated potential for inferring molecular phenotypes from routine hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) whole-slide images (WSIs), current architectures primarily rely on vision-centric self-supervised learning or vision-language alignment, lacking the spatially resolved molecular supervision required to connect subtle morphological features with underlying genomic alterations. Spatial transcriptomics (ST) emerges as a transformative technology that enables transcriptomic quantification within intact tissue sections, thereby preserving the precise spatial link between histology and molecular profiles. In this study, we present a Spatial Transcriptomics-guided Alignment framework for Molecular Profiling (STAMP), which endows PFMs with intrinsic molecular awareness. To support this paradigm, we curated HumanST-1k, a human ST dataset spanning diverse anatomical organs and sequencing platforms. This atlas yields 1.8 million pairs of H&E patches and corresponding transcriptomic profiles, providing a corpus that links histological structures with their molecular states. To mitigate the technical noise inherent to raw transcriptomics, STAMP applies a pathway-informed alignment strategy that aggregates transcriptomic data into biologically functional pathways, which are subsequently integrated into PFMs via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. This alignment enriches the representation space of PFMs and unlocks their capacity to resolve sub-visual molecular signatures. The clinical utility of these augmented representations was validated through a multi-tier evaluation framework.

</details>


### [211] [The Shape of Addition: Geometric Structures of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03645)
*Liuyuan Wen,Xun Zhu,Lihao Huang,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析残差流几何结构，发现大语言模型在算术中的错误源于内部连续进位潜势受噪声干扰跨越量化阈值导致的"几何滑移"，并提出基于几何一致性的检测与修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在基础算术任务中表现出矛盾性的脆弱（paradoxical fragility），暗示其内部计算与离散输出之间存在脱节，亟需从几何角度解释这一现象。

Method: 分析多操作数加法中残差流的几何结构，识别出等原始和轨迹（IRST）；提出带噪量化模型（Noisy Quantization Model），将算术错误建模为内部神经噪声推动连续进位潜势跨越量化阈值导致的几何滑移（Geometric Slippages）。

Result: 揭示了IRST几何结构（语义数字锚定+连续进位纤维调制）；阐明了探针通用性的几何基础；提出了一种几何一致性检查方法，能有效检测和纠正推理过程中的量化失败。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一解释了算术错误、探针通用性等现象，并提供了实用的错误检测与纠正方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit paradoxical fragility in fundamental arithmetic, implying a disconnect between internal computation and discrete output. By analyzing the residual stream geometry during multi-operand addition, we identify the Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST), a geometric structure where representations are anchored by semantic digits and modulated by continuous carry fibers. We propose the Noisy Quantization Model to explain this geometry, framing arithmetic errors as Geometric Slippages caused by internal neural noise pushing a continuous, latent Carry Potential across quantization thresholds. This geometric framework further elucidates Probe Versatility, explaining how lightweight probes can disentangle coexisting latent signals (such as ground truth versus hallucination) from a single activation vector. Finally, we validate these insights through a geometric consistency check method that effectively detects and corrects these quantization failures during inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/RL-MIND/Shape-of-Addition.

</details>


### [212] [Speedrunning Tabular Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03681)
*Salih Bora Ozturk,Alexander Pfefferle,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对表格基础模型 nanoTabPFN 的社区预训练速度竞赛（speedrun），通过单文件训练脚本和固定目标（下游 ROC AUC），在单张 L40S GPU 上实现了从 74.32 分钟到 0.92 分钟的 81 倍加速，同时减少了 22 倍的合成数据集使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练成本是表格基础模型研究的主要瓶颈，减缓了新架构、先验和优化思想的迭代周期，而社区缺乏简单的方式来比较和累积预训练加速效果。

Method: 设计了一个社区 speedrun 格式：贡献者修改单文件训练脚本，在 subsampled TabArena 上使用单张 NVIDIA L40S GPU 竞争达到固定的下游 ROC AUC 目标。

Result: 当前最佳记录在 0.92 分钟内达到目标，相比基线 74.32 分钟实现了 81 倍加速，同时使用了 22 倍更少的合成数据集。

Conclusion: 该 speedrun 格式为社区提供了一种简单、可验证、可叠加的预训练改进协议，并开放了排行榜供贡献。

Abstract: Pretraining cost is a major bottleneck for research on tabular foundation models, slowing the iteration cycle for new architectures, priors, and optimization ideas. Yet the community lacks a simple way to compare and accumulate pretraining speedups. We introduce a community speedrun for nanoTabPFN: contributors modify a single-file training script and compete to reach a fixed downstream ROC AUC target on subsampled TabArena using one NVIDIA L40S GPU. The current best record reaches the target in 0.92 minutes, an 81x speedup over the 74.32 minute baseline while using 22x fewer synthetic datasets. The speedrun format provides a simple protocol for the community to add, verify, and stack pretraining improvements, with the leaderboard open to contributions. Code and records are available at https://github.com/borawhocodess/modded-nanotabpfn.

</details>


### [213] [A Close Look At World Model Recovery In Supervised Fine-Tuned LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03685)
*Patrick Emami,Nan Qiang,Peter Graf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在经典规划任务上对微调LLM进行可解释性实验，发现监督微调使模型线性编码动作有效性和状态谓词，随机游走数据能更好地恢复世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索微调后的LLM在解决经典规划问题时是否真正学习到了表征和推理规划问题的知识，而不仅仅是生成动作序列。

Method: 设计了一系列可解释性实验，从内部表征和生成能力两个角度全面检验微调LLM的世界模型恢复情况。

Result: a) 监督微调使LLM线性编码动作有效性和部分状态谓词；b) 无法用输出概率分类动作有效性的模型仍可能内部表征区分有效与无效动作；c) 更广的状态空间覆盖（如随机游走数据）能更准确恢复世界模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为规划型LLM的可解释性分析提供了方法论，并揭示了LLM中知识表征的开放问题。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves end-to-end classical planning in large language models (LLMs), but do these models also learn to represent and reason about the planning problems they are solving? Due to the relative complexity of classical planning problems and the challenge that end-to-end plan generation poses for LLMs, it has been difficult to explore this question. In our work, we devise and perform a series of interpretability experiments that holistically interrogate world model recovery by examining both internal representations and generative capabilities of fine-tuned LLMs. We find that: a) Supervised fine-tuning on valid action sequences enables LLMs to linearly encode action validity and some state predicates. b) Models that struggle to use output probabilities for classifying action validity may still learn internal representations that separate valid from invalid actions. c) Broader state space coverage during fine-tuning, such as from random walk data, yields more accurate recovery of the underlying world model. In summary, this work contributes a recipe for applying interpretability techniques to planning LLMs and generates insights that shed light on open questions about how knowledge is represented in LLMs.

</details>


### [214] [Staying Alive: Uncensored Survival Analysis with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03689)
*Mariana Vargas Vieyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表格基础模型（TFM）的无训练生存回归方法，通过迭代插补右删失数据来构建加速失效时间（AFT）模型，在无需训练的条件下与需训练的生存回归模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中的右删失问题限制了表格基础模型在时间-事件数据上的应用，现有TFM缺乏针对删失数据的处理能力。

Method: 利用TFM预测事件时间并迭代插补右删失数据；构建仅需拟合单一标量参数的AFT模型；基于Buckley-James估计器提出非参数上下文估计器。

Result: 在标准生存分析基准上，该方法与Cox回归、参数化AFT等需训练的模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 提出的无训练方法有效拓展了TFM在生存分析中的应用，为删失数据处理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Survival Analysis (SA) is a statistical framework that models the time span until some event of interest occurs. Widely used in several domains, including healthcare and churn prediction, a central challenge in its applicability stems from the time of the event being partially observed or \emph{right-censoring}.
  Tabular Foundation Models (TFM) have attracted significant interest in recent years due to their ability to perform prediction tasks in a single forward pass, requiring no dataset-specific parameter fitting. Despite their success, their application to prediction tasks on time-to-event data remains difficult due to right censoring. In this work, we present a training-free method to survival regression by leveraging TFMs to both predict the time of the event and iteratively impute right-censored data.
  Our method uses a TFM to construct an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model requiring no training beyond fitting a single scalar parameter. Subsequently, by building on the Buckley-James estimator, we introduce a non-parametric in-context estimator for right-censored data. Our experiments on standard survival analysis benchmarks show that our method is competitive with several parametric and semi-parametric survival regression models that require training, including Cox regression and parametric AFT models.

</details>


### [215] [Multi$^2$: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Decision-Making with LLM-Based Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03698)
*Sangeun Park,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi²，一种分层多智能体决策框架，将LLM智能体行为分解为高层代理（System 1，基于SFT生成子目标）和低层代理（System 2，通过离线到在线RL执行原子动作），以缓解长时间交互中的目标漂移问题，并在多个交互环境中优于强基线，同时发布了三个分层基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长程决策中容易发生目标漂移（objective drift），即目标与计划在长时间交互中逐渐偏离，导致决策不稳定。

Method: 提出分层多智能体框架Multi²，明确将行为分解为互补角色：高层代理（System 1）通过监督微调（SFT）生成上下文感知的子目标，低层代理（System 2）通过离线到在线强化学习（RL）在交互环境中执行原子动作。

Result: 在多个交互环境中，Multi² consistently优于强智能体基线，展现出更强的鲁棒性和协调性。同时构建并发布了三个分层基准数据集，填补了训练与评估分层决策的空缺。

Conclusion: 这种角色分离能够实现稳定的长程控制，缓解目标漂移，并支持高效的适应能力。

Abstract: A central goal of large language model (LLM) research is to build agentic systems that can plan, act, and adapt through sustained interaction with dynamic environments. While recent LLM-based agents exhibit impressive contextual reasoning, their long-horizon decision-making remains fragile, often suffering from objective drift, where goals and plans drift over extended interactions. We introduce Multi$^2$, a hierarchical multi-agent decision-making framework that explicitly decomposes agent behavior into complementary roles. A high-level agent (System 1) focuses on context-aware sub-goal generation using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while a low-level agent (System 2) executes atomic actions through offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) in interactive environments. This separation enables stable long-horizon control, mitigates objective drift, and allows efficient adaptation. Across diverse interactive environments, Multi$^2$ consistently outperforms strong agentic baselines, demonstrating improved robustness and coordination in multi-turn interaction. Beyond performance, we introduce and release three hierarchical benchmark datasets, filling a long-standing gap in training and evaluating hierarchical decision-making for LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [216] [When Graph Tokens Sink: A Mechanistic Analysis of Graph Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03712)
*Ding Zhang,Runtao Zhou,Wenqing Zheng,Rizal Fathony,Bayan Bruss,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析图语言模型（GLM）中图令牌的内部行为，发现图令牌存在激活值异常高的"图汇令牌"，但这些令牌并非图信息的真正载体，激活显著性并不等价于图语义实用性，表明当前GLM中图令牌的构建、放置和对齐机制存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM内部如何处理图令牌，以及图令牌是否真正承载了图结构信息，揭示激活显著性与图语义效用之间的解耦现象。

Method: 通过分析图令牌内部显著性、激活值分布、注意力权重，以及进行剪枝、重定位和交换干预实验来研究图令牌的行为。

Result: 图汇令牌在激活层面表现为离群值（少量隐藏维度上激活值极大），但不一定吸引最大注意力权重；通过干预实验证明图汇令牌并非对下游预测最重要的语义或结构令牌。

Conclusion: 当前GLM将图结构映射到LLM令牌空间后，生成的图令牌表示并未自然形成可用的拓扑感知内部表示，激活显著性与图语义效用之间存在解耦，表明现有图令牌构建机制存在局限。

Abstract: Graph Language Models (GLMs) have become a promising direction for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to graph learning tasks. By transforming graph topology and node information into graph tokens, GLMs allow LLMs to jointly process structured graph inputs and textual instructions. Yet, it remains unclear how LLMs internally interpret these graph tokens and whether graph tokens act as meaningful carriers of graph structure. In this work, we analyze how LLMs process graph information through graph-token behavior in representative GLM architectures.
  Findings. We find that the internal saliency of graph tokens in GLMs is not equivalent to graph information utilization. Graph sink tokens consistently emerge as activation-level outliers: they can be identified by massive activation values along a small set of hidden-state dimensions and are biased toward early graph-token positions. However, this activation-level saliency does not imply that these tokens are the main carriers of graph information. Unlike classical attention sinks in language and vision-language models, graph sink tokens do not necessarily attract the largest attention weights from query tokens. Through pruning, repositioning, and swapping interventions, we show that graph sink tokens are not the most important semantic or structural tokens for downstream prediction.
  Implications. Together, these results suggest that after current GLMs map graph structure into the LLM token space, the resulting graph-token representations do not naturally form a fully usable topology-aware internal representation; instead, they exhibit a decoupling between activation-level saliency and graph-semantic utility. This decoupling points to limitations in existing graph-token construction, placement, and alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [217] [Compress then Merge: From Multiple LoRAs into One Low-Rank Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03723)
*Zhengbao He,Ruiqi Ding,Zhehao Huang,Ruikai Yang,Tao Li,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Compress-then-Merge (CtM)方法，通过先压缩后合并的逆序流程，在合并多个LoRA前强制施加秩约束，避免了传统Merge-then-Compress方法中全参数合并破坏低秩结构的问题，在保证秩约束的同时提升了合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Merge-then-Compress方法先在全参数空间合并LoRA再压缩，这一过程可能破坏低秩结构，导致后续SVD截断难以恢复有效的低秩LoRA，从而影响合并后适配器的性能。

Method: CtM采用"先压缩后合并"的反向流程：首先利用LoRA权重计算共享的r维子空间以捕捉跨适配器的共性结构，然后将每个适配器投影到共享子空间得到r×r的坐标表示，最后在缩减空间中应用标准合并规则。该方法从根本上保证了合并结果为秩r的LoRA，无需后验截断。

Result: 在多个模型和任务上的实验表明，CtM一致优于已有的单LoRA输出基线方法，同时缩小了与全参数合并方法之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: CtM通过重构合并流程，在合并前强制施加秩瓶颈约束，既保证了低秩结构的完整性，又实现了高效的跨适配器合并，为LoRA的高效复用和部署提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient specialization of foundation models, but the proliferation of task-specific adapters fragments capabilities across many adapters, complicating reuse and deployment. We study the problem of merging $T$ LoRAs into a single rank-$r$ LoRA, thereby preserving the benefits of low-rank structure. Existing Merge-then-Compress pipelines treat the rank constraint as an afterthought: they merge adapters in the full parameter space, then compress the merged result to rank $r$ via truncated SVD. However, full-parameter merging may destroy the low-rank structure, making it difficult for subsequent compression to recover an effective rank-$r$ LoRA. We propose Compress-then-Merge (CtM), a reversed pipeline that enforces the rank-$r$ bottleneck before merging: CtM computes shared $r$-dimensional subspaces using only the LoRA weights to capture cross-adapter common structure, projects each adapter into the shared subspaces to obtain $r\times r$ coordinates, and then applies standard merging rules in this reduced space. CtM guarantees a rank-$r$ LoRA by construction, avoiding post-hoc truncation, and enables efficient computation in the core space spanned by concatenated LoRA factors. Experiments across multiple models and tasks show that CtM consistently outperforms existing single-LoRA-output baselines while narrowing the performance gap to full-parameter merging methods.

</details>


### [218] [Conformal Language Modeling via Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03731)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Theo X. Olausson,Armando Solar-Lezama,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM后验近似的采样方法，通过在条件序列生成中校准高得分区域来实现风险控制，相比事后过滤（如共形预测）能生成更连贯、一致且有用的文本，在传记生成和数学问题求解任务上取得了同等统计保证但更高的下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共形预测的事后过滤方法存在三个问题：1）将采样视为原子操作，事后修改会导致文本不连贯、不一致或不符合模型分布；2）无法将概率质量转移到更有用、更有帮助的回答上。因此需要一种将校准与生成过程相结合的方法。

Method: 从LLM后验的近似分布中采样，其中条件事件对应一个经过校准的高得分区域。作者针对条件序列生成场景设计了专门的校准过程，以有效识别该区域并实现目标风险控制。

Result: 在开放域传记生成和数学问题求解两个案例中，与先前工作相比，该方法在提供相同统计保证的同时，获得了更高的下游效用。

Conclusion: 将校准融入生成过程而非事后过滤，可以在保持统计保证的前提下提升生成质量，是一种更优的幻觉控制范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models remain plagued by hallucinations. Recent work has sought to tame their prevalence using statistical techniques based on conformal prediction, with both theoretical and empirical success. However, these methods operate in a post-hoc fashion, treating the sampling procedure itself as atomic and then surgically altering samples to remove hallucinated claims. This disconnect between filtering and generation can result in samples that are incoherent, inconsistent, or simply unlikely under the model itself. Moreover, post-hoc surgery is unable to shift probability mass towards more useful and helpful responses. To address these issues, we propose to instead sample from approximations to an LLM posterior, where the conditioning event corresponds to a calibrated, high-scoring region. We develop a calibration procedure tailored to the setting of conditional sequential generation that effectively identifies this region and achieves target risk control. Empirically, we apply our method to case studies focused on open-ended biography generation and mathematical problem solving; compared to prior work, we obtain the same statistical guarantees, with higher downstream utility.

</details>


### [219] [Tool-Aware Optimization with Entropy Guidance for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03762)
*Hongye Cao,Nuo Yan,Haoyuan Deng,Ziwei Wang,Tianpei Yang,Jing Huo,Yuyao Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAO-RL提出了一种统一框架，通过工具感知轨迹过滤和熵引导探索相结合，解决大语言模型在强化学习中集成外部工具导致的训练不稳定问题，在7个推理基准上取得优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将外部工具集成到LLM的RL训练中会导致训练不稳定：过度依赖工具引发输入分布偏移，而过度保守使用工具则限制了有效探索。

Method: TAO-RL包含两个核心组件：(1) 数据层面的工具感知轨迹过滤，根据两条标准丢弃退化轨迹——所有工具调用都失败的轨迹，以及所有rollout全对或全错的轨迹；(2) 算法层面的工具感知熵引导奖励，在工具调用后的token位置重塑优势函数，鼓励策略在关键决策点探索更多样化的推理路径。

Result: 在7个具有挑战性的推理基准上、跨越3种模型规模的大量实验表明，TAO-RL优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹过滤与熵引导探索两个组件相互强化：前者建立干净且信息丰富的训练基础，后者在关键工具交互节点推动更强的推理行为，从而有效解决了工具集成带来的训练不稳定问题。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) equips large language models (LLMs) with tool-use capabilities that substantially improve reasoning on complex tasks. However, integrating external tools often destabilizes training: over-reliance on tools can induce input distribution shift, while overly conservative tool use limits effective exploration. To address this issue, we propose a unified framework TAO-RL that couples tool-aware trajectory filtering with entropy-guided exploration for efficient policy optimization. Specifically, at the data level, TAO-RL filters rollout trajectories along two criteria: discarding those where all tool invocations fail to execute, and removing those where all rollouts are either correct or incorrect, as both cases yield degenerate advantage estimates that contribute no discriminative learning signal. This joint filtering retains data that are both tool-capable and informative, establishing a high-quality training distribution. At the algorithmic level, we introduce a tool-aware entropy-guided bonus that reshapes the advantage function at post-tool-call tokens, encouraging the policy to explore more diverse reasoning paths at critical decision points. These two components are mutually reinforcing: trajectory filtering establishes a clean and informative training foundation, while entropy-guided exploration drives stronger reasoning behaviors at critical tool-interaction junctures. Extensive experiments on 7 challenging reasoning benchmarks across 3 model scales demonstrate the superiority of TAO-RL over existing methods.

</details>


### [220] [Limit Analysis of Graph Neural Networks with Wireless Conflict Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03794)
*Romina Garcia Camargo,Zhiyang Wang,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了基于稀疏随机几何图（RGG）构建的图神经网络（GNN）在不同规模无线网络间的可迁移性，通过建立RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的逼近关系，给出了策略迁移时的性能损失上界，并在链路调度任务中验证了所提方法在大规模场景下优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络资源分配中，GNN虽展现出从小规模图向大规模图迁移的能力，但现有理论分析多针对一般图结构，缺乏对无线网络特有的稀疏性（节点连接数少）和几何随机性（基于RGG建模）的理论保证。

Method: 聚焦于RGG生成的冲突图（用于建模链路间干扰），通过刻画RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的结构逼近关系，推导出GNN模型跨规模迁移时性能损失的理论上界。

Result: 在链路调度问题中，基于该理论框架学习到的策略在大规模部署下始终优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文为GNN在稀疏无线网络中的可迁移性提供了理论依据，验证了理论假设在实践中的有效性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for wireless resource allocation that leverages the underlying graph structure of communication networks. Their transferability property enables models trained on small-scale graphs to generalize to large-scale deployments with little performance deterioration, a desirable property for currently growing networks. Wireless networks are sparse regimes, where a single node is connected to a small number of other users. This work establishes theoretical results for transferability of GNNs over graphs derived from sparse Random Geometric Graphs (RGGs). In particular, we focus on conflict graphs of RGGs used to model interference among links. Our approach considers the closeness between RGGs and Deterministic Grid Graphs (DGG) to establish bounds in the performance loss when a model is transferred across scales. We validate our theoretical findings through the problem of link scheduling, demonstrating that our learned policies consistently outperform existing benchmarks at scale. Finally, we examine the impact of our theoretical assumptions on empirical performance.

</details>


### [221] [Trading Human Curation for Synthetic Augmentation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03800)
*Akshansh,Leonardo Rosa Rodrigues,Michael Korostelev,Youssef Hassan,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，使用预定义、门控过滤的增强任务替代人工策划任务的可行性与成本效益。通过成本调整的交换率ρ_cost衡量增强任务与人工任务之间的替代关系，实验表明在十项基准测试中，增强内容可保持与额外人工任务相当的泛化性能，成本交换率在1.4倍至11.6倍之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练任务的供应是RLVR在语言智能体模型上的核心瓶颈。每个任务需要沙盒环境、提示和人工编写的奖励函数，且只有通过质量门槛的任务才能产生有效训练信号。手工策划在经济上无法扩展到RL有效训练所需的任务数量，而自动生成的任务变体与人工任务之间的替代率尚未明确。

Method: 使用预定义、门控过滤的增强策略对少量人工编写的基础任务进行扩充，作为RLVR中额外人工策划的替代方案。形式化定义了成本调整的交换率ρ_cost，通过控制训练语料库中增强任务占比进行消融实验测量该比率，并在十项基准测试上评估端到端泛化性能。

Result: 增强内容替代额外人工任务在代码、指令遵循、推理和多轮智能体函数调用等十项基准测试中保持了聚合的保留集泛化性能。成本调整的交换率ρ_cost在合理的c_human/c_aug范围内保持在[1.4×, 11.6×]之间。

Conclusion: 预定义门控过滤的增强任务可以作为RLVR中额外人工策划的经济有效替代方案，在保持泛化性能的同时显著降低任务生成成本。

Abstract: The supply of high-quality training tasks is a central bottleneck for reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on agentic language models. Each task requires a sandboxed setup, a prompt, and a hand-authored reward function, and only tasks that pass a quality bar produce useful training signal. Hand-curation at this quality bar does not scale economically to the task counts effective RL training requires, and the substitution rate between automatically generated task variants and human-authored ones is not yet established. We investigate using pre-specified, gate-filtered augmentations of a small hand-authored base as a substitute for additional human curation during RLVR. We formalize the cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between augmented and human-authored tasks, measure it through a controlled ablation across training corpora with varying augmentation share, and characterize the end-to-end economics of the augmentation pipeline. Substituting augmented content for additional human-authored tasks retains aggregate held-out generalization on a ten-benchmark suite spanning code, instruction following, reasoning, and multi-turn agentic function-calling. The cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between gated synthetic and human-authored RLVR tasks stays in $[1.4\times, 11.6\times]$ across the plausible $c_{\text{human}}/c_{\text{aug}}$ range.

</details>


### [222] [Easy-to-Use Shielding for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03804)
*Stefan Pranger,Bettina Könighofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍 tempestpy，一个将形式化盾牌综合工具 Tempest 集成到 Gymnasium API 的 Python 库，使得强化学习中的安全探索（屏蔽技术）能够作为端到端组件被 RL 从业者直接使用；同时扩展了 Tempest 对随机多人博弈的盾牌计算支持，并引入 MiniGridSafe 环境集以促进实验可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏蔽技术（shielding）虽然能够为 RL 提供形式化的安全保证，但由于需要形式化方法专业知识且工程集成成本高，在实际 RL 工作流中很少被采用。现有缺乏可访问的端到端基础设施将形式化盾牌综合与标准 RL 框架连接起来。

Method: 1) 开发 tempestpy Python 库，将盾牌综合工具 Tempest 封装为 Gymnasium API 兼容的后端，实现 shield 的自动合成与部署。2) 扩展 Tempest 算法，支持随机多人博弈（stochastic multiplayer games）的 sound shield 计算，保持形式化安全保障。3) 设计 MiniGridSafe 环境集合，在 MiniGrid 基础上增加概率转移和多智能体等安全导向场景。

Result: 提供了端到端的工作流演示，并在多个环境中对比了有 shield 和无 shield 的 RL 训练效果。给出了符号化 MiniGrid 模型，并通过 MiniGridSafe 使屏蔽过程更易访问和实验透明。

Conclusion: tempestpy 显著降低了 RL 中使用形式化屏蔽技术的门槛，使安全探索方法成为 RL 从业者可用的实用组件。对随机多人博弈的扩展拓展了形式化安全保证的适用范围。

Abstract: Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Shielding is one such technique that assumes domain knowledge in the form of an environment model to decide upon action safety. Although well-established, shielding has seen limited adoption in RL due to the lack of accessible end-to-end infrastructure connecting formal shield synthesis with standard RL frameworks. Applying shielding typically requires expertise in formal methods and substantial engineering effort, keeping it outside the typical RL workflow. We address this by extending our shield synthesis tool Tempest into a practical backend for safe RL.
